### [1] [Toward High-Fidelity 3D Point-Cloud Learning for Brain Folding Morphology Prediction Using Trans-Unet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21840)
*Geran Zhao,Xiaotian Li,Poorya Chavoshnejad,Mir Jalil Razavi,Akbar Solhtalab,Lijun Yin,Guifang Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Trans-Unet框架，通过将3D点云数据转换为2D网格域，并采用结合CNN和自注意力机制的U形混合模型，有效学习高分辨率3D点云特征，用于预测大脑折叠模式，在保真度和准确性上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云特征学习面临排列不变性、缺乏局部上下文、细粒度表面重建困难和高计算成本等挑战，现有方法难以同时兼顾局部细节与全局语义。

Method: 提出Trans-Unet框架：1) 将3D点云数据转换为2D网格域，降低维度灾难和计算成本；2) 采用U形混合架构，集成CNN捕获层次化局部表示，自注意力机制建模全局语义和长距离依赖。

Result: 在包含40,401个表面点和2,382个纤维点的高分辨率脑补丁点云数据上，Trans-Unet实现了从初始状态到最终状态的大脑折叠高精度预测，在保真度和准确性上超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 3D到2D的变换保留了细粒度结构信息并降低计算成本，CNN与自注意力的结合有效平衡了局部与全局特征学习，验证了混合架构在3D点云特征学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Learning high-fidelity point-cloud features in the 3D space poses significant challenges, including permutation invariance, lack of local context, difficulty in fine-grained surface reconstruction, and high computational cost. In this article, we propose Trans-Unet, a novel framework that addresses these issues by first tansforming 3D point-cloud data into a 2D grid domain and then employing a U-shaped hybrid model that integrates Convolutional Neural Networks, and self-attention mechanisms. The proposed Trans-Unet effectively learns and reconstructs precise features from high-resolution 3D point-cloud data (with 40,401 points in surface and 2,382 points in fiber) derived from a predefined finite element brain patch growth model, enabling accurate prediction of brain folding patterns. By combining multiple techniques, Trans-Unet leverages the complementary strengths: the 3D-to-2D transformation preserves fine-grained structural information while significantly reducing computational cost and the curse of dimensionality; convolutional blocks capture hierarchical, low-level local representations; and the self-attention mechanism models global, high-level semantics and long-range dependencies. The dataset consists of 3D point-clouds containing both brain surface patches and fiber information generated by a large-scale finite element model. Trans-Unet is applied to predict brain surface folding from the initial state (state 0 or states 0-2) to the final state (state 3). Experimental results demonstrate that Trans-Unet achieves high-resolution predictions of brain patch growth, surpassing existing methods in both fidelity and accuracy.

</details>


### [2] [Farmland Extent and Visible Boundary Mapping from 1 m NAIP Imagery Using Residual U-Net and Text-Prompted SAM 3 Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21881)
*Mohammadreza Narimani,Vikram Anand,Parastoo Farajpoor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于ResUNet与冻结SAM 3融合的可复现工作流，利用1米NAIP RGB影像进行农田范围与可见边界提取，在测试集上达到IoU 0.8605、Dice 0.9234的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业用地图往往专有、不完整或过时，但这些地图是作物监测、产量核算和土地转化分析的空间基础。因此需要一种可复现的自动化方法从遥感影像中提取农田范围与边界。

Method: 从37张1米NAIP RGB影像中标注了5,698个256×256图块（3,850训练/770验证/1,078测试）。采用残差U-Net（ResUNet）搭配Dice主导损失L=2.5(1-Dice)+BCE进行训练，并与冻结的SAM 3（提示词"agricultural farmland field"）通过逻辑或（OR）进行融合。滑动窗口拼接生成连贯的区域掩膜。

Result: 测试集准确率0.8808、IoU 0.8605、Dice 0.9234、精确率0.8766、召回率0.9794。与SAM融合后，困难图块上Dice从0.858提升至0.955（果园行）和0.804至0.903（破碎地块）。示例区域掩膜Dice达0.898和0.919。

Conclusion: 生成的语义农田范围图层可作为当前耕地图层不可用时的农业监测支撑，但其为语义层面而非地籍地块级别。

Abstract: Agricultural field maps are often proprietary, incomplete, or outdated, yet they provide the spatial framework for crop monitoring, production accounting, and land-conversion analysis. This study presents a reproducible workflow for mapping farmland extent and visible boundaries from 1 m NAIP RGB imagery. Thirty-seven scenes spanning open cropland, peri-urban interfaces, semi-arid irrigation geometries, and fragmented mosaics were annotated in CVAT and converted to binary masks. Non-overlapping 256 x 256 patches yielded 5,698 samples, split by source scene into 3,850 training, 770 validation, and 1,078 test patches. A residual U-Net (ResUNet) trained with a Dice-dominant loss, L = 2.5(1 - Dice) + BCE, achieved test accuracy 0.8808, IoU 0.8605, Dice 0.9234, precision 0.8766, and recall 0.9794. A frozen SAM 3 branch prompted with "agricultural farmland field" was fused with ResUNet by logical OR. On selected difficult patches, Dice improved from 0.858 to 0.955 (orchard rows) and from 0.804 to 0.903 (fragmented parcels). Sliding-window stitching produced coherent regional masks (example tile Dice 0.898 and 0.919). The product is a semantic farmland-extent layer, not a cadastral parcel map, and supports agricultural monitoring where current field layers are unavailable.

</details>


### [3] [Oxygen-TryOn: Fashion-Native Foundation Model for Any-item Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21694)
*Yong Liu,Xiaolong Fu,Zihang Xu,Wen Xue,Xueheng Li,Lin Song,Yuan Zhang,Chuyang Zhao,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Yipeng Sun,Yan Li,Simiu Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Oxygen-TryOn是一个专为任意商品虚拟试穿设计的统一基础模型，通过专门的数据引擎和三阶段训练（CPT、SFT、RL），支持多参考图像、多类别服装、全身/半身视图以及自由组合，在单件和多件试穿任务上达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟试穿系统大多局限于单一服装类别和工作室场景，多参考方法仍以服装为中心，缺乏支持任意品类、任意数量参考图、自由组合的统一框架。

Method: 构建时尚原生的基础模型，通过专用数据引擎大规模收集、制造、标注和筛选高质量试穿数据；采用三阶段训练策略：继续预训练（CPT）、监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）；RL阶段使用混合奖励模型（内部试穿奖励模型+专有准则引导通用模型）联合监督细粒度一致性和指令级质量。

Result: 在公开基准和内部Oxygen-TryOn Bench上，单件试穿达到SOTA，多件试穿领先，匹配或超越Nano Banana Pro、GPT-Image-2、Seedream5 Lite等商业系统及FLUX.2等开源模型。

Conclusion: 将虚拟试穿从基于掩码的修补问题重新定义为多参考、理解驱动的生成任务，证明了专用数据引擎和定制化训练范式在构建通用试穿模型中的有效性。

Abstract: We present Oxygen-TryOn, a unified foundation model for any-item virtual try-on. Rather than repurposing a general-purpose image editor, Oxygen-TryOn is fashion-native, built for try-on through a dedicated data engine and try-on-specific training. Given one or more reference items (clean product shots or in-the-wild worn-on photos) and a single target subject image, it synthesizes a photorealistic image of the subject wearing the items across virtually any fashion category. Prior systems handle a single garment category in a studio setting, and recent multi-reference methods remain garment-centric; in contrast, Oxygen-TryOn supports diverse items and scenarios, including full- and half-body views, a variable number of references, and free multi-item composition, while faithfully preserving both subject identity and item appearance. Instead of mask-based inpainting, we reformulate try-on as a multi-reference, understanding-driven generation task. We build a data engine that collects, manufactures, annotates, and filters high-quality try-on data at scale, and design a three-stage recipe of continued pre-training (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). The RL stage uses a hybrid reward combining an in-house try-on reward model with a proprietary, rubric-guided general-purpose model, jointly supervising fine-grained consistency and instruction-level quality. It also follows general editing instructions (e.g., pose changes) in the same pass. Across public benchmarks and our in-house Oxygen-TryOn Bench, it achieves state-of-the-art consistency and realism on single-item try-on and leads on multi-item try-on, matching or surpassing both leading proprietary systems (Nano Banana Pro, GPT-Image-2, Seedream5 Lite) and open-source models (FLUX.2).

</details>


### [4] [Time-Reversed Imaging: A Multimodal Benchmark and Framework for Reconstructing Past Human-Environment Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22352)
*Jorge Bacca,Kebin Contreras,Luis Toscano-Palomino,Mauro Dalla Mura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了时间反演成像（time-reversed imaging）这一新范式，旨在从热、紫外和可见光等多模态衰落痕迹中推断场景中刚刚发生的过去事件。作者构建了首个概念验证数据集TRACE-HEI，包含同步三模态视频序列，并提出了多模态推理方法，利用视觉-语言引导的扩散模型重建过去帧。实验表明该任务具有挑战性但可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频理解关注当前或未来帧的预测，而人类与环境交互后会在物理表面留下随时间衰减的多模态痕迹（如热残留、紫外指纹、可见光印记）。现有方法无法从这些衰落痕迹中逆向推断过去事件，本文旨在填补这一空白，建立时间反演成像的计算与实验基础。

Method: 1) 构建TRACE-HEI数据集，包含热、紫外、可见光三模态同步视频，涵盖坐、触摸、移动物体、液体泼洒等动作，记录接触后长达三分钟的多材料表面痕迹。2) 提出多模态推理方法：先提取痕迹的结构化文本描述，再以该描述约束视觉-语言引导的扩散模型，用于重建合理的过去帧。

Result: 从衰落痕迹推断近期事件具有挑战性但可行；互补的多模态信息能够有效降低解的不确定性，提升过去帧重建的质量。

Conclusion: 本文首次定义了时间反演成像的计算与实验框架，桥接了视觉、物理与生成式推理，为超越瞬时观测的场景理解开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce time-reversed imaging, a new paradigm that infers what just happened in a scene from fading multimodal traces. Instead of extrapolating or interpolating video frames, our goal is to infer past human-environment interactions from residual physical imprints observable in thermal, ultraviolet, and visible spectra. To study this problem, we present TRACE-HEI, the first proof-of-concept dataset for time-reversed imaging, containing synchronized tri-modal video sequences of actions such as sitting, touching, moving objects, and liquid spills, captured across diverse materials and recorded up to three minutes after contact. To establish the benchmark, we propose a multimodal inference approach that extracts structured textual descriptions of detected traces and uses them to constrain a vision-language-guided diffusion model for reconstructing plausible past frames. Experiments show that inferring recent events from fading traces is challenging but feasible when complementary modalities reduce solution ambiguity. This work defines the first computational and experimental foundation for time-reversed imaging, bridging vision, physics, and generative reasoning, and opening new directions for scene understanding beyond instantaneous observation.

</details>


### [5] [Be Consistent! Enhancing Robust Visual Reasoning in LVLMs with Consistency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21722)
*Liqiang Jing,Xiong Zhou,Siddharth Varia,Neha Anna John,Xinya Du,Vassilis N. Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConVBench基准（六类视觉推理任务，含逻辑等价问题对）和ConVLM方法（基于GRPO强化学习与一致性奖励），用于评估和提升LVLM的视觉推理逻辑一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM在视觉推理任务中存在脆弱性，且现有基准多聚焦符号数学或简单视觉任务，缺乏对复杂视觉推理和逻辑一致性的评估，而这正是可靠推理系统的关键要求。

Method: 1) 构建ConVBench基准：每张图片配两个逻辑等价问题，涵盖动作状态、复杂计数、空间推理、因果意图、常识推理、时间感知六类；2) 定义逻辑一致性和鲁棒准确率两个评估指标；3) 提出ConVLM方法：基于GRPO强化学习，引入自动生成的逻辑等价问答对和双重奖励（准确率+一致性），鼓励配对回答保持一致。

Result: 提出新的基准和评估指标，ConVLM方法在有无严格答案监督下均能有效提升LVLM的逻辑一致性表现。

Conclusion: 通过逻辑等价问题对和一致性强化学习，可以显著改善LVLM在复杂视觉推理任务中的逻辑一致性，为构建更可靠的视觉推理系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) exhibit strong perceptual capabilities, they remain vulnerable in visual reasoning tasks. Existing benchmarks largely focus on symbolic mathematical or scientific problems and simple vision-centric tasks, offering limited assessment of complex visual reasoning and logical consistency, a critical requirement for reliable reasoning systems. We introduce ConVBench, a complex vision-centric reasoning benchmark in which each image is paired with two logically equivalent questions across six categories: action and state, complex counting, spatial reasoning, causal and intent understanding, commonsense reasoning, and temporal perception. To complement this benchmark, we define two evaluation metrics, logical consistency and robust accuracy, that jointly assess both the correctness and consistency of model responses. We further present ConVLM, which improves LVLM reasoning through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)-based reinforcement learning with a novel consistency reward. This method leverages automatically generated logically equivalent question-answer pairs and a dual-reward design combining accuracy- and consistency-based signals, encouraging agreement between paired responses. The framework functions effectively with or without strict answer supervision.

</details>


### [6] [Risk-Routed Implicit Boundary Refinement for Robust Ultrasound Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21787)
*Jingguo Qu,Xinyang Han,Xiang Wang,Yuqi Yang,Tonghuan Xiao,Sheng Ning,Jing Qin,Ann Dorothy King,Winnie Chiu-Wing Chu,Jing Cai,Michael Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Risk-routed Implicit Boundary Refinement (RIBR)，一种紧凑的超声图像分割框架，将隐式神经表示作为风险路由的残差校正而非无约束的全掩膜预测。通过边界细化隐式残差、风险路由残差控制以及几何和散斑感知的边界正则化，在九个超声数据集上实现了最优的整体宏平均性能并一致降低了边界误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声图像分割面临散斑噪声、低对比度边界、声影和采集差异等挑战，现有编码器-解码器和Transformer网络虽性能强劲，但仍可能产生过度平滑的轮廓或在分布偏移下不稳定预测。

Method: 提出RIBR框架，包含三个核心组件：边界细化隐式残差（Boundary-refinement Implicit Residuals）、风险路由残差控制（Risk-routed Residual Control）以及几何与散斑感知的边界正则化（Geometry- and Speckle-aware Boundary Regularization），将隐式神经表示作为风险路由的残差校正项而非全掩膜预测器。

Result: 在九个超声数据集（涵盖淋巴结、乳腺病变、甲状腺结节和前列腺）上进行评估，RIBR在紧凑参数预算下取得了最优的整体宏平均性能，并在分组和器官特定比较中一致降低了边界误差。

Conclusion: 受控的隐式残差学习是资源受限且边界敏感的超声分割任务中一种实用策略。

Abstract: Medical ultrasound (US) image segmentation faces significant challenges due to speckle noise, low-contrast boundaries, acoustic shadowing, and acquisition variation across operators and clinical centers. Although encoder-decoder and transformer-based networks have achieved strong performance, many methods recover boundary details through dense decoders or larger backbones, which may still produce over-smoothed contours or unstable predictions under external distribution shifts. In this article, we propose Risk-routed Implicit Boundary Refinement (RIBR), a compact segmentation framework that uses implicit neural representation as a risk-routed residual correction rather than an unconstrained full-mask predictor. RIBR combines boundary-refinement implicit residuals, risk-routed residual control, and geometry- and speckle-aware boundary regularization to refine uncertain contours while suppressing non-boundary oscillations. Evaluation on nine US datasets covering lymph nodes, breast lesions, thyroid nodules, and prostate shows that RIBR achieves the best overall macro-average and consistently reduces boundary error across grouped and organ-specific comparisons under a compact parameter budget. These findings suggest that controlled implicit residual learning is a practical strategy for resource-constrained and boundary-sensitive US segmentation. Source code is available at https://github.com/jinggqu/ribr.

</details>


### [7] [What Happens to Accuracy When Photo Lineups Contain Non-Mated Rank-One Images From Large Galleries?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21792)
*Genesis Argueta,Kevin W. Bowyer,Michael King,Jayeeta Dhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了一对多人脸识别在执法中的应用问题，发现更大的目标库规模会显著增加证人错误识别率及其错误信心，并指出该流程已导致至少9起冤假错案，质疑了将此类识别结果用于照片列队指认及作为逮捕依据的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对目标库规模如何影响证人从照片列队中做出错误识别的系统量化分析，且该问题直接关系到人脸识别技术在执法环节中导致的冤假错案风险。

Method: 通过实验比较三种不同规模目标库（500、5,000和24,000张图像）下，证人从照片列队中做出正确与错误识别的准确率及信心水平。

Result: 更大的目标库规模同时增加了证人做出错误识别的概率及其对该错误识别的信心。

Conclusion: 研究结果对当前执法实践中将人脸识别检索结果用于照片列队指认的流程提出质疑，并进一步质疑仅凭照片列队指认结果是否应构成逮捕的合理依据。

Abstract: One-to-many facial identification is commonly used to match a probe image from surveillance video against a gallery of driver's licenses and/or booking photos. The algorithm's rank-one image from the gallery, or a human examiner's selection from the algorithm's top-ranked images, may then be placed in a photo lineup shown to a witness. Witness selection of the gallery image in the photo lineup may then lead directly to the person in the gallery image being arrested. This facial identification process is involved in at least 9 wrongful arrests. This work specifically examines whether the probability of a witness making an incorrect identification increases with the size of the gallery searched. We compare photo lineup accuracy when the "suspect" image is drawn from galleries of 500, 5,000, and 24,000 images. We find that larger galleries increase both the likelihood of a witness making an incorrect identification and their confidence in that (incorrect) identification. These results raise questions of whether an image resulting from such a facial identification process should be used in photo lineups and of whether results of a photo lineup alone should constitute probable cause for arrest.

</details>


### [8] [Closing the Loop: Training-Free Revisit Consistency for Autoregressive Generative Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21848)
*Wenchao Ma,Changran Liu,Sharon X. Huang,Haomiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需后训练的方法，通过利用3D引擎提供的时空对应关系（时间对应检索历史潜变量块、空间对应通过相机位姿和深度重投影引导注意力），解决自回归视频生成中相机重访时因KV缓存过期导致的外观不一致问题，在TartanAir/TartanGround数据集上优于现有训练自由基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成模型在长序列生成中，当相机重访已超出KV缓存范围的场景区域时，即使深度等条件渲染与几何完美对齐，模型仍会生成不一致的外观（即重访不一致问题）。现有方法需要后训练或微调，缺乏轻量级解决方案。

Method: 1) 时间对应：利用3D引擎提供的时序对应关系，将位姿匹配的历史潜变量块检索回KV缓存，作为闭环记忆。2) 空间对应：基于相机位姿和深度重投影，在token级别将注意力偏置到检索块中几何对应的区域。无需任何后训练。

Result: 在TartanAir和TartanGround数据集的闭环轨迹上验证，方法在重访一致性指标上优于现有训练自由基线，同时保持整体视频质量不下降。

Conclusion: 利用3D引擎天然提供的时空对应关系，无需额外训练即可有效解决自回归视频生成中的重访不一致问题，为游戏和沉浸式内容创作中的长序列生成提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Recent conditional video generation models have shown promising potentials to transform 3D engine renderings, such as depth maps and untextured geometry, into photorealistic videos for gaming and immersive content creation. These applications require long-horizon auto-regressive generation that continuously synthesizes new frames while preserving a persistent 3D world. Auto-regressive generators synthesize video chunk by chunk with a bounded KV cache, so when the camera revisits a location after its context has been evicted, the model often regenerates inconsistent appearance, even though the conditioning renderings (e.g., depth) remain perfectly aligned with the underlying geometry.We address this revisit inconsistency without any post-training by exploiting correspondences the 3D engine already provides: temporal correspondence retrieves pose-matched historical latent chunks into the KV cache as loop-closure memory, while spatial correspondence from camera pose and depth reprojection biases token-level attention toward geometrically corresponding regions of the retrieved chunks. We demonstrate our method on loop-closure trajectories mined from TartanAir and TartanGround dataset to mirror complicate real-world application scenarios, where it outperforms existing training-free baselines on revisit consistency without losing overall video quality. Project Page: https://wenchao-m.github.io/ClosetheLoop.github.io/

</details>


### [9] [SCALE: Self-Supervised Constraint-Aware Layout GEneration for Local P&R DRV Fixing at Advanced Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21850)
*Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Guanglei Zhou,Yoshi Nishi,Yaguang Li,Walker Turner,Cunxi Yu,Yiran Chen,Brucek Khailany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCALE框架通过自监督布局生成和领域自适应VLM，在亚2nm节点上将局部DRC违反修复成功率提升12-25%（最高达97%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着半导体制造推进至亚2nm节点，局部布局布线（P&R）的DRC违反修复受到复杂规则交互、密集多层布线几何和晶圆厂特定约束的严重限制。现有LLM在EDA脚本和文档方面表现优异，但缺乏对视觉布局理解（尤其是DRC违规诊断）所需的精确几何推理和晶圆厂特定规则知识。

Method: 提出SCALE框架，包含两阶段：1) 自监督布局生成阶段：将多层布局几何序列化为结构化文本，微调语言模型学习从周围BEOL上下文中重建随机掩蔽的多边形（无需违反标签）；2) 推理阶段：自然语言规则约束结合高温采样生成多样化的违规倾向布局变体，经工业级DRC验证器标注后微调领域自适应DRC-VLM，提供规则感知的几何指导用于局部DRV修复。

Result: 在100个真实亚2nm案例（涵盖包围、间距、宽度和颜色间距违规）上，将最先进代理的求解率提升12-25%，最高达到97%。

Conclusion: SCALE证明了自监督布局生成与领域自适应VLM相结合的方法能有效解决先进节点DRC违规修复难题，为LLM/VLM在物理设计自动化中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: As semiconductor manufacturing advances toward sub-2nm nodes, local place-and-route (P&R) design-rule violation (DRV) fixing is increasingly limited by complex rule interactions, dense multi-layer routing geometries, and foundry-specific constraints. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in EDA scripting and documentation, their application to visual layout understanding remains largely unexplored: diagnosing DRC violations from layout imagery demands precise geometric reasoning and foundry-specific rule knowledge absent from general-purpose VLM training. We propose SCALE, a framework with a self-supervised layout-generation stage for local DRV fixing at advanced nodes. Multi-layer layout geometry is serialized into structured text, and a fine-tuned language model learns to reconstruct randomly masked polygons from surrounding BEOL context alone without violation labels. At inference, natural-language rule constraints and high-temperature sampling steer generation toward diverse, violation-prone layout variants validated by an industrial signoff DRC checker, producing DRC-annotated layout--violation pairs used to fine-tune a domain-adapted DRC-VLM. This VLM provides rule-aware geometric guidance for local DRV repair, boosting state-of-the-art agents' solve rates by +12--25% (up to 97%) on 100 real sub-2nm cases spanning enclosure, spacing, width, and color-spacing violations.

</details>


### [10] [Learning Adaptive Semantic Gaussian Allocation for 3D Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21896)
*Kanglin Ning,Yiran Zhao,Wenrui Li,Houde Quan,Qifan Li,Xingtao Wang,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAGFormer，通过显式容量分配机制解决语义3D高斯表示中的分配瓶颈问题，在有限高斯数量下实现更优的语义占用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义3D高斯方法聚焦于原语的表示、初始化或增密方式，但未显式解决在总高斯数量受限（为控制内存和计算）时如何选择最有用的高斯原语，导致简单区域高斯冗余而困难区域语义支持不足的分配瓶颈。

Method: 提出语义高斯分配Transformer（SAGFormer），利用高斯属性与局部几何-语义特征对候选高斯进行评分，并选择固定的最终高斯集合。

Result: 在nuScenes-SurroundOcc和SSCBench-KITTI-360数据集上，SAGFormer提升了占用预测性能，产生语义更一致、利用率更高的高斯表示；在相似最终数量和原始覆盖下，减少了语义混合，增强了类别一致的体素支持，产生了更少的未使用高斯。

Conclusion: 显式容量分配是高斯精化在语义占用预测中的有效补充。

Abstract: Semantic 3D Gaussians provide a compact representation for 3D semantic occupancy prediction by rendering semantic primitives into a voxel volume under voxel-wise supervision. Recent methods have improved the modeling ability and efficiency of this representation through more flexible primitive shapes, geometry-guided initialization, and progressive densification. However, these advances mainly determine how primitives are represented, initialized, or added, and do not explicitly address how to select the most useful Gaussians when their total number must be limited to control memory and computation. This imbalance creates an allocation bottleneck: redundant Gaussians remain in simple regions, while difficult regions receive insufficient semantic support. We propose the Semantic Gaussian Allocation Transformer (SAGFormer), which uses Gaussian attributes and local geometric-semantic features to score candidates and select a fixed final Gaussian set. Experiments on nuScenes-SurroundOcc and SSCBench-KITTI-360 show that SAGFormer improves occupancy prediction under the evaluated protocols and yields more semantically consistent and better-utilized Gaussian representations. Under similar final counts and raw coverage, it reduces semantic mixing, strengthens class-consistent voxel support, and produces fewer unused Gaussians. The results indicate that explicit capacity allocation is a useful complement to Gaussian refinement for semantic occupancy prediction.

</details>


### [11] [ISPCloak: Weaponizing ISP for Optimization-Free Physical Camouflage against Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21897)
*Jiale Zhao,Jiajun Wan,Lei Tang,Ye Qin,Kebing Jin,Jinghui Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ISPCloak，一种无需优化的对抗攻击框架，通过向AI生成图像注入真实相机ISP管线的物理噪声统计特征，使多种深度伪造检测器失效，揭示了现有检测范式忽略了物理成像特征的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器专注于捕捉数字合成伪影，但当AI生成内容被赋予真实物理成像特征时，检测性能急剧下降。论文旨在揭示这一盲点并利用物理成像特征设计攻击方法。

Method: 采用可逆ISP网络将图像投影到RAW域，注入逼真的泊松-高斯传感器噪声，执行前向ISP重建，结合生成伪影抑制和自适应掩码，无需梯度扰动即可生成对抗样本。

Result: 广泛实验表明，嵌入真实物理扰动能够在视觉几乎不变的情况下，使多种当前检测机制普遍失效，生成的对抗样本具有高度逃逸性。

Conclusion: 当前深度伪造检测范式存在根本盲点，即忽略了硬件固有的物理成像统计特征。物理仿真攻击为评估检测器鲁棒性提供了新视角，亟需发展能捕获物理成像特征的新检测方法。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative models has spurred the critical need to evaluate the worst-case robustness of deepfake detectors. In this paper, we reveal a fundamental blind spot in current forensic paradigms: while existing detectors excel at capturing digital synthesis artifacts, their effectiveness drops drastically when AI-generated content is cloaked in authentic physical imaging characteristics. We posit that genuine photographs inherently possess hardware-intrinsic statistical signatures, which are imperceptible footprints imprinted by optical sensors and Image Signal Processing (ISP) pipelines, and are fundamentally absent in purely data-driven generative models. Driven by this insight, we propose ISPCloak, a novel optimization-free adversarial attack framework that explicitly weaponizes the ISP pipeline to mislead the judgment of deepfake detectors. Rather than relying on computationally expensive gradient perturbations, our method first employs an Invertible ISP network to project images into the RAW domain. Then, we seamlessly imprint the complex statistical priors of real cameras onto AI-generated images by injecting realistic Poisson-Gaussian sensor noise and conducting forward ISP reconstruction. Synergized with generative artifact suppression and adaptive masking, this streamlined physical simulation enables ultra-fast generation of adversarial examples. Extensive experiments show that embedding authentic physical perturbations fundamentally disrupts a broad range of current detection mechanisms, yielding universally evasive adversarial examples with imperceptible visual alterations.

</details>


### [12] [Diffusion Models in Medical Image Inpainting: Challenges, Solution Taxonomy, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21904)
*Arthur Dantas Mangussi,Joana Cristo Santos,Ricardo Cardoso Pereira,Ana Carolina Lorena,Mário A. T. Figueiredo,Pedro Henriques Abreu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于扩散模型的医学图像修复方法，涵盖60项研究，分析了主流架构（DDPM、LDM）、应用场景（伪影去除、数据增强、伪健康组织重建、异常检测）及主要挑战（缺乏标准化基准、数据集多样性有限、验证程序不足）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像中的伪影、信息缺失和病理改变可能损害诊断可靠性，扩散模型因其生成解剖一致性重建的能力成为有前景的解决方案，但缺乏系统性的综述来梳理该领域的发展现状和挑战。

Method: 对60项研究进行系统综述，提出扩散方法分类体系，并从架构、应用、数据集、评估策略四个维度进行分析。

Result: 扩散模型在生成解剖学合理的重建和辅助下游临床任务方面表现优异；DDPM和LDM是主导架构；研究主要集中在MRI和CT成像的伪影去除、数据增强等任务。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在医学图像修复中展现出强大潜力，但领域仍面临标准化基准缺失、数据多样性不足、临床验证不充分等挑战。

Abstract: Image inpainting aims to reconstruct missing or corrupted regions of an image while preserving as much as possible, visual and semantic consistency. In medical imaging, this task is particularly important because artifacts, missing information, and pathological alterations can compromise diagnostic reliability and downstream clinical applications. Recently, diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative approaches for medical image inpainting due to their ability to generate anatomically consistent reconstructions. This survey presents a systematic review of diffusion-based methods for medical image inpainting, covering the main architectures, applications, datasets, and evaluation strategies reported across 60 studies. In addition, we propose a taxonomy for diffusion-based approaches. The analysis reveals a rapid growth of research interest in diffusion-based medical image inpainting, with denoising diffusion probabilistic models and latent diffusion models emerging as the dominant architectures. The reviewed studies mainly focus on artifact removal, data augmentation, pseudo-healthy tissue reconstruction, and anomaly detection, particularly in magnetic resonance imaging and computed tomography imaging. Overall, diffusion models demonstrate strong performance in producing anatomically plausible reconstructions and aiding downstream clinical tasks. However, the review also highlights important challenges, including the lack of standardized benchmarks, limited dataset diversity, and restricted validation procedures across diverse clinical applications and imaging scenarios.

</details>


### [13] [Low-Altitude Channel Multipath Prediction via Panoramic Perception and Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21953)
*Zihang Zeng,Shu Sun,Meixia Tao,Zhiyong Chen,Jianhua Mo,Xiangwen Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PanoLAMP框架，利用全景RGB-D观测和预训练视觉-语言模型预测无人机通信中的低空多径参数（时延、功率、角度偏移），在合成数据集上优于现有基线方法，并展现出良好的跨高度泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计信道模型在特定环境下精度有限，确定性方法（如射线追踪）依赖精确三维环境模型且计算复杂度高，现有多模态信道预测方法主要关注路径损耗等大尺度指标，对小尺度参数建模不足。

Method: PanoLAMP框架：以预训练视觉-语言模型为骨干，通过在收发两端采集全景RGB-D观测来捕获传播环境特征，预测相对于视距路径的时延、功率、方位角和天顶角偏移。

Result: 在包含18949条无人机-车辆链路、覆盖7种无人机高度的合成数据集上，PanoLAMP在多径参数和统计指标上均优于代表性基线，且在不同飞行高度上展现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PanoLAMP利用视觉-语言模型和全景感知实现了低空多径参数的有效预测，解决了传统方法在精度和计算复杂度之间的权衡问题，为6G无人机通信中的信道建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) communication is expected to support a wide range of low-altitude applications in 6G mobile networks. However, traditional statistical channel models provide limited accuracy in specific environments, while deterministic methods such as ray tracing usually rely on accurate three-dimensional environment models and involve high computational complexity. Existing multimodal channel prediction approaches mainly focus on large-scale metrics such as path loss, and remain insufficient for modeling small-scale parameters. To address these limitations, this paper proposes PanoLAMP, a Panoramic perception and vision-language model-based Low-Altitude Multipath Prediction framework. It adopts a pretrained vision-language model as the backbone and captures the propagation environment features through panoramic RGB-D observations collected at both the transmitter and receiver to predict the delay, power, azimuth angle, and zenith angle offset relative to the line-of-sight path. Experiments are conducted on a synthetic dataset containing 18,949 UAV-vehicle links across seven UAV altitudes. Experimental results show that the proposed method consistently outperforms representative baselines in both multipath parameters and statistical metrics, and demonstrates stronger generalization across different flight heights.

</details>


### [14] [TextSLIP: Text Self-Supervised CLIP for Medical Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21970)
*Haoyu Jiang,Ziping Cong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextSLIP框架，通过在CLIP中引入文本侧自监督对比学习（文本对增强），为视觉编码器提供更细粒度的语义监督，在7百万脑MRI图像-文本对预训练后，在报告生成任务上优于标准CLIP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP方法缺乏细粒度语义监督，仅优化跨模态对齐而未显式构建指导视觉表征学习的文本嵌入空间。

Method: TextSLIP在CLIP基础上增加模态内文本对比学习模块，通过自监督增强文本对提升文本嵌入的可判别性，从而为视觉编码器提供更精细的语言监督。

Result: 在7百万脑MRI图像-文本对预训练后，TextSLIP在报告生成指标上持续优于CLIP基线；消融实验证实文本侧自监督是性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 文本级对比学习是改善医学视觉-文本对齐的有效方向，但需在更多医学领域进行验证。

Abstract: Automating radiology report generation is important for improving reporting consistency and clinical workflows . While Contrastive Language--Image Pretraining (CLIP) has advanced medical vision language modeling, existing CLIP-style approaches may still provide insufficient fine-grained semantic supervision for complex report generation. Standard CLIP primarily optimizes cross-modal alignment, without explicitly structuring the textual embedding space that guides visual representation learning. To address this limitation, we propose TextSLIP, a general medical vision-language pretraining framework that augments CLIP with intra-modal text contrastive learning. By improving textual embedding discriminability through self-supervised augmented text pairs, TextSLIP is designed to provide finer-grained linguistic supervision to the visual encoder. As an initial validation, we pretrain TextSLIP on a curated dataset of 7 million brain MRI image-text pairs and fine-tune the pretrained visual encoder within a report generation architecture. In controlled comparisons with CLIP-style baselines, TextSLIP shows consistent improvements on report generation metrics. Ablation studies further suggest that text-side self-supervision contributes to the observed gains. These results indicate that text-level contrastive learning is a promising direction for improving medical visual-textual alignment, while broader validation across additional medical domains remains an important next step.

</details>


### [15] [Rethinking Layer-Wise Information Allocation for Vision Foundation Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21973)
*Yuqi Li,Xi Xiao,Yunbei Zhang,Lin Zhao,Yu Li,Aiden Zhao,Tianyang Wang,Hao Xu,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出提示信息瓶颈（PIB）框架，基于信息瓶颈原理对视觉基础模型进行参数高效适配，通过层级压缩-充分性正则化实现任务相关信息的保留与冗余信息的丢弃，在34个数据集上以平均0.35%可调参数取得SOTA性能，并提升了模型鲁棒性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的适配方法（如VPT）在层间行为上缺乏理解：性能对提示深度、位置和任务分布敏感，且域内性能提升未能转化为鲁棒泛化。作者认为这不仅是优化问题，更是层级信息分配问题——缺乏对提示条件表示在跨层间应保留、抑制和传播什么的原则性控制。

Method: 受信息瓶颈（IB）原理启发，提出提示信息瓶颈（PIB）框架。核心思想是适配应满足最小充分性：早期层保留任务相关的局部证据，深层逐步丢弃干扰因素与冗余细节。通过正则化层间压缩-充分性权衡，构建更连贯的跨层信息路径。

Result: 在34个数据集上取得优异结果：FGVC达92.1%，HTA达93.01%，VTAB-1k达77.33%，平均仅调优0.35%参数。此外，PIB有助于解释提示容量缩放的非单调行为、减少捷径学习，并提升分布偏移和细粒度识别下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PIB不仅是一种实用的参数高效适配方法，更提供了一个从信息分配视角理解冻结视觉基础模型适配的新框架。

Abstract: Vision foundation models are increasingly reused as frozen backbones for downstream visual recognition, making parameter-efficient adaptation a central problem. Prompt-based adaptation, including Visual Prompt Tuning (VPT), provides a lightweight way to specialize these models, but its layer-wise behavior remains poorly understood: performance is sensitive to prompt depth, placement, and task distribution, and gains on standard in-domain benchmarks do not always translate into robust generalization. We argue that this limitation is not solely an optimization issue, but a layer-wise information allocation issue: existing prompt-based methods lack principled control over what prompt-conditioned representations should preserve, suppress, and propagate across depth. Inspired by the Information Bottleneck principle, we introduce Prompted Information Bottlenecks (PIB), a framework that regularizes layer-wise compression-sufficiency trade-offs and promotes a more coherent cross-layer information path. The key idea is that effective adaptation should be minimal yet sufficient, retaining task-relevant local evidence in earlier layers while progressively discarding nuisance factors and redundant details in deeper layers. Extensive experiments show that PIB achieves strong performance across 34 datasets, reaching 92.1% on FGVC, 93.01% on HTA, and 77.33% on VTAB-1k, while tuning only 0.35% parameters on average across the main settings. Beyond benchmark accuracy, PIB helps explain the non-monotonic behavior of prompt capacity scaling, reduces shortcut reliance, and improves robustness under distribution shift and fine-grained recognition settings. These results position PIB as both a practical method and an information-allocation perspective for adapting frozen vision foundation models. Our code is available at https://github.com/itsnotacie/MM-26-PIB

</details>


### [16] [Medical-Checklist: Assessing the Comprehension of Medical Images by Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21998)
*Bannapol Limanond,Masanori Suganuma,Takayuki Okatani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Medical-Checklist基准测试，通过二选一图文匹配任务评估医学多模态模型是否真正理解图像内容，发现现有SOTA模型虽在特定任务表现优异，但对图像的理解仍不准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学多模态模型在特定任务（如Med-VQA）上表现良好，但缺乏统一、简洁的评估方法来判断模型是否真正理解图像与文本之间的关联。需要一种能减少数据偏差、支持分布外评估的基准测试。

Method: 构建Medical-Checklist数据集，设计二选一任务：给定一张图像和两个描述文本（一个正确，一个错误），错误描述仅替换一个医学概念（词或短语）。该设置统一了不同原理的多模态模型评估，并能检验模型对多种医学子领域中广泛医学概念的理解。

Result: 对四个SOTA医学多模态模型的评估显示，尽管它们在Med-VQA等任务上表现出色，但在Medical-Checklist的简单二选一任务中暴露了对图像理解的不足。

Conclusion: 当前医学多模态模型对图像的正确理解仍有很大差距，距离临床应用还有很长的路要走。Medical-Checklist为未来医学多模态模型的诊断性评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new benchmark test, Medical-Checklist, for assessing medical multimodal models. The recent advancements in multimodal models have demonstrated significant potential in the field of medical vision-language tasks. However, it is becoming increasingly clear that evaluating these models' performance, whether they are applied to natural or medical images, is challenging. The critical question is whether the models can accurately understand an input image while associating it with relevant input text. To address this, Medical-Checklist imposes a binary test on the models: they are given an image and two captions, where one is correct and the other incorrect, and the model must select the correct one. The incorrect caption contains a single medical concept (word or phrase) that is inaccurately substituted from the correct caption. Although the task is simple, this simplicity enables the unified assessment of diverse multimodal models designed and learned on different principles. It also enables us to verify whether models correctly understand a wide range of medical concepts across various medical sub-domains. Medical-Checklist is designed to reduce potential biases in data and to enable evaluation of the models' ability to handle out-of-distribution inputs, which were difficult in existing datasets. When evaluating four state-of-the-art medical multimodal models with Medical-Checklist, it was revealed that despite their excellent performance in specific tasks such as Med-VQA, they may not correctly understand images, suggesting a long journey ahead for clinical application. The dataset and code will be made public upon acceptance.

</details>


### [17] [Visual Saliency Steering Distillation for Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22013)
*Hao Yang,Jin Wang,Xuejie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VSSD是一种视觉显著性引导蒸馏方法，通过利用多模态大模型的注意力图生成扰动图像，再经奇异值分解提取主导引导向量进行层间蒸馏，以增强小模型在多模态思维链推理中对微小跨模态差异的感知能力，在ScienceQA和M³CoT上提升了理由生成和答案推理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型在有限的token预算下，模态交互融合常压制微小跨模态差异，导致不同图像搭配相同文本或不同文本搭配相同图像时难以区分，影响多模态思维链推理质量。

Method: 利用多模态大语言模型的注意力图生成扰动图像，捕获任务敏感的特征方向；再通过奇异值分解提取主导引导向量，用于指导层间蒸馏。

Result: 在ScienceQA和M³CoT数据集上，VSSD显著提升了理由生成和答案推理的性能。

Conclusion: VSSD通过视觉显著性引导蒸馏有效解决了小模型在多模态思维链推理中跨模态差异被抑制的问题，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning integrates visual and textual cues through step-by-step inference. In small models with limited token budgets, modality-interaction fusion often suppresses tiny cross-modal differences. In particular, multimodal CoT often struggles when different images pair with identical text or different texts pair with an identical image, making such inputs nearly indistinguishable after fusion. This study proposes Visual Saliency Steering Distillation (VSSD). VSSD leverages the attention maps of multimodal large language models to generate perturbed images that capture task-sensitive feature directions, and then applies singular value decomposition to extract dominant steering vectors to guide inter-layer distillation. Experiments on ScienceQA and M$^3$CoT demonstrate that VSSD improves rationale generation and answer inference. The code is available at https://github.com/BGWH123/VSSD.

</details>


### [18] [EVL-MCoT: Enhanced Vision-Language Multi-CoT for Harmful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22016)
*Hao Yang,Jin Wang,Xuejie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强型视觉语言多链式思维（EVL-MCoT）方法，通过多视角推理、原型引导和上下文引导的解码框架，解决了现有MEME理解方法中背景信息缺失、浅层特征融合和细粒度视觉-文本对齐不足的问题，在HatefulMemes和MultiOff数据集上取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MEME理解方法采用双流视觉语言模型同时提取视觉和文本信息，但缺乏背景知识和先验知识；简单的链式思维（CoT）方法缺乏多视角思考，导致答案可靠性不足；且依赖浅层特征融合，缺乏局部细节与细粒度视觉提示文本对齐能力。

Method: 提出增强型视觉语言多链式思维（EVL-MCoT）方法，通过多链式思维促进决策一致性并减少偏差；设计原型引导和上下文引导的解码框架，引入视觉原型指导融合过程，实现文本与视觉信息的精确对齐。

Result: 在HatefulMemes和MultiOff数据集上取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: EVL-MCoT通过多视角推理和细粒度视觉-文本对齐，有效提升了MEME中隐含含义的理解能力。

Abstract: MEMEs are widely used on the internet and often carry strong elements of sarcasm or irony. Understanding their hidden meanings typically requires a joint interpretation of text and vision. Existing methods focus on the dual-stream vision-language model to extract the visual and text simultaneously, which lacks background information and prior knowledge about the comprehensive explanation of MEME. One feasible option is to adopt chain-of-thought (CoT). However, the simple CoT approach lacks multi-perspective thinking, which may compromise the reliability of the resulting answers. Moreover, it often relies on shallow feature fusion, lacking the fusion of local details and fine-grained visual-prompt text alignment. This limitation prevents a deeper understanding of the intricate connections between the visual and the text. Herein, an enhanced vision-language multi-CoT (EVL-MCoT) approach is proposed to address these limitations. By promoting multi-CoT, EVL-MCoT enhances consistency and reduces bias in the decision-making process. Additionally, we design a prototype-guided and context-guided decoding framework, which incorporates visual prototypes to guide the fusion process and enables the model to align textual and visual information more precisely. We achieve promising results on the HatefulMemes and MultiOff datasets. The source code has been publicly released and is available at https://github.com/BGWH123/EVL-MCoT.

</details>


### [19] [Small Vision-Language Models Know When They Are Wrong But Cannot Say So: A Two-Model Study of Stated versus Internal Confidence Under Realistic Image Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22034)
*M M Asif Ferdous*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了两个小型VLM（Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct和SmolVLM-Instruct）在六种真实图像退化条件下的不确定性信号表现。发现：模型的语言化置信度几乎恒定（Qwen2-VL均值0.87-0.90），错误检测能力接近随机水平（AUROC约0.50）；而内部token概率则能有效区分正确/错误回答（AUROC 0.92-0.99）。两种信号在严重欠曝条件下均失效。结论：小型VLM编码了可用的自我知识但未通过语言化输出表达，内部概率是更好的延迟决策信号，两种信号在低光照条件下均不可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在消费硬件上部署时面临图像退化问题，可靠的置信度信号比准确率更重要，因为它决定了系统何时应延迟回答而非直接输出。

Method: 在六种真实摄影退化（压缩、抖动、弱光等）的三个严重级别下，对比两种置信度信号：模型自然语言陈述的置信度（语言化置信度）和模型生成回答的平均token概率（内部概率），使用AUROC评估错误检测能力。

Result: Qwen2-VL的语言化置信度几乎恒定（0.87-0.90），错误检测为随机水平（AUROC约0.50），而内部概率AUROC达0.92-0.99。SmolVLM的语言化置信度基本无法获取，内部概率AUROC为0.54-0.92。严重欠曝条件下，准确率暴跌（0.99→0.22），但两种置信度信号几乎不变，内部错误检测降至随机水平。

Conclusion: 小型VLM编码了可用的自我知识但未通过语言化输出表达，内部概率是更好的延迟决策信号；两种信号在严重低光照条件下均不可信。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed on consumer hardware where input images are degraded by compression, camera shake, and poor lighting. In such settings, a reliable uncertainty signal matters more than raw accuracy, because it determines when a system should defer rather than answer. We evaluate two small open-weight VLMs -- Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct and SmolVLM-Instruct -- across six realistic photographic degradations at three severity levels, comparing two confidence signals: the confidence the model states in natural language, and the model's own mean token probability over its generated answer. Across 3,800 predictions, we find a large and consistent gap. Verbalized confidence in Qwen2-VL is almost constant (mean 0.87-0.90 across all conditions) and detects its own errors at chance level (AUROC 0.39-0.75, typically ~0.50), while internal token probability from the same model separates correct from incorrect answers with AUROC 0.92-0.99. In SmolVLM, verbalized confidence proved largely unobtainable: across three prompt templates, only one of five pilot attempts produced a parseable confidence value, while internal probability again yielded above-chance error detection (AUROC 0.54-0.92). Both models fail in the same place: under severe underexposure, accuracy collapses (0.99->0.22 for Qwen2-VL, 0.97->0.42 for SmolVLM) while both confidence signals barely move, and internal error-detection falls to chance. We conclude that small VLMs encode usable self-knowledge that their verbalized output does not express, that internal probability is therefore the better deferral signal in constrained deployment, and that neither signal should be trusted under severe low-light conditions.

</details>


### [20] [A Smooth Phase-Separation Model for Weak-Boundary Segmentation of Homogeneous Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22053)
*Zihan Li,Jiebao Sun,Fanghui Song,Zhichang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Cahn-Hilliard方程的平滑相分离变分模型，用于弱边界下同质外观结构的分割，通过软最大区域拟合与相场正则化结合，并设计了混合L²-H⁻¹梯度流和稳定化SAV数值格式，实验证明该方法在弱边界分割上优于经典变分、相场及深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相邻结构在强度分布相似且边界弱/模糊时，经典变分模型因图像驱动力退化，易出现边界泄漏或相邻区域错误合并，亟需一种能保持界面判别力的分割方法。

Method: 1) 提出基于Cahn-Hilliard方程的平滑相分离变分模型；2) 集成softmax区域拟合与Cahn-Hilliard相场正则化；3) 引入混合L²-H⁻¹梯度流以保持高阶界面正则化并允许相质量自适应变化；4) 建立连续能量耗散律并证明弱解存在唯一性；5) 开发线性、基于FFT的稳定化SAV数值格式，满足修正离散能量耗散律。

Result: 在合成图像和医学图像上的实验表明，该方法能有效分离弱边界下的相邻同质结构，在分割精度和边界定位上优于代表性变分、相场及深度学习方法。

Conclusion: 该模型在弱/模糊边界分割任务中具有显著优势，兼顾了高阶正则化与计算效率，为同质结构分割提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: Segmentation of adjacent structures with similar intensity distributions remains a challenging problem in image analysis, particularly when object boundaries are weak or ambiguous. Under such conditions, classical variational models may suffer from degenerated image-driven forces, leading to boundary leakage or undesired merging of neighboring regions. To address these limitations, we propose a smooth phase-separation variational model based on the Cahn--Hilliard equation for weak-boundary segmentation of homogeneous-appearance structures. The proposed framework integrates softmax-based region fitting with Cahn--Hilliard phase-field regularization to maintain interface discrimination under weak image-driven forces. We further introduce a mixed $L^2-H^{-1}$ gradient flow, which preserves higher-order interfacial regularization while allowing adaptive changes of phase masses, establish the continuous energy dissipation law, and prove the existence and uniqueness of weak solutions in the natural solution class. For numerical computation, we develop a stabilized scalar auxiliary variable (SAV) scheme that is linear, FFT-based, and satisfies a modified discrete energy dissipation law. Numerical experiments on synthetic and medical images demonstrate that the proposed method effectively separates adjacent homogeneous structures across weak boundaries and achieves competitive segmentation accuracy and improved boundary localization compared with representative variational, phase-field, and deep learning methods.

</details>


### [21] [Rethinking Multi-Branch and Cross-Backbone Fusion for Vehicle Re-Identification in the Foundation-Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22068)
*Yu Wang,Hongyu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究发现，在基础模型时代，使用单一DINOv3预训练的ConvNeXt骨干网络配合精心调优的训练策略，即可达到甚至超越多分支架构和CNN-Transformer融合方法的性能。多分支融合带来的性能提升不足1 mAP点，而Transformer分支比ConvNeXt骨干低13-15 mAP。结论是：改进单一强基础模型骨干网络结合检索阶段重排序，比增加架构复杂度更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分支架构和CNN-Transformer融合长期被认为是提升车辆重识别性能的有效方法，但在基础模型时代这些假设是否仍然成立尚不明确。本文旨在通过系统的实证研究重新审视这一假设。

Method: 采用DINOv3预训练的ConvNeXt作为单骨干基线，结合调优训练策略和免训练重排序；设计检索级分支诊断工具，评估多分支（基于共享骨干的拼接）和跨骨干融合（非对称冻结锚定策略融合ConvNeXt与Vision Transformer）的性能增益；采用逐查询配对bootstrap分析估计融合增益的置信区间。

Result: 单骨干ConvNeXt在VeRi-Wild Small上达88.19 mAP，Large上达77.47 mAP，重排序后分别提升至92.38和83.68 mAP。多分支融合改变最佳单分支性能不足1 mAP点，嵌入维度增加4倍。交叉骨干融合中Transformer分支比ConvNeXt低13-15 mAP，最大观测融合增益仅+0.11 mAP（95%置信区间）。

Conclusion: 在评估设定下，改进单一强基础模型骨干网络并配合检索阶段重排序，比通过增加分支或异构骨干提升架构复杂度更有效。结论局限于单种子训练和一类基础模型家族，并讨论了这些观察可能不成立的条件。

Abstract: Multi-branch architectures and CNN-Transformer fusion have long been regarded as effective ways to improve vehicle re-identification (Re-ID) by combining complementary representations. In this work, we revisit this assumption in the foundation-model era through a comprehensive empirical study. A single DINOv3-pretrained ConvNeXt trained with a tuned recipe achieves 88.19 mAP on VeRi-Wild Small and 77.47 mAP on VeRi-Wild Large using visual cues alone, matching the strongest protocol-verified metadata-dependent multi-branch baseline. Applying training-free re-ranking further improves performance to 92.38 and 83.68 mAP, respectively. Using this strong baseline together with retrieval-level branch diagnostics, we evaluate whether increasing representational diversity still provides measurable gains. Across both benchmarks, concatenating multiple branches built on a shared backbone changes the best single-branch performance by less than one mAP point while increasing the embedding dimension by 4x, and the resulting representation has an effective rank close to the original feature dimension. We further study cross-backbone fusion using an asymmetric frozen-anchor strategy to combine ConvNeXt and Vision Transformer representations. Despite these favorable conditions, Transformer branches consistently remain 13-15 mAP below the ConvNeXt backbone, and paired per-query bootstrap analysis estimates the largest observed fusion gain to be only +0.11 mAP (95% confidence interval). Our results suggest that, under the evaluated setting, improving a single strong foundation-model backbone together with retrieval-stage re-ranking is more effective than increasing architectural complexity through additional branches or heterogeneous backbones. We restrict our conclusions to single-seed training and one family of foundation models and discuss conditions under which these observations may not hold.

</details>


### [22] [ReCowGnition: A Realistic Biometric Benchmark for Cow Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22071)
*Marco Huber,Marco Kiesewalter,Judith Louise Pieper,Bastian Kubsch,Naser Damer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含6,838张161头奶牛面部图像的真实场景公开数据集，定义了2个验证和4个识别评估协议，并评估了6种基准模型（包括小样本训练、跨物种微调和零样本基础模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对视觉动物生物特征识别在精准畜牧业中的应用需求，解决复杂场景、相似外观、遮挡、非合作行为以及公开标注数据集有限等挑战。

Method: 在真实自动化场景中采集奶牛面部图像，构建包含6,838张161头奶牛的数据集；定义2个验证和4个识别评估协议；对6种基准模型（小样本训练、跨物种微调、零样本基础模型）进行评估。

Result: 成功构建了公开可用的奶牛面部基准数据集，并提供了六种基准模型在不同评估协议下的性能结果。

Conclusion: 该数据集和评估协议为奶牛识别研究提供了标准化基准，促进了计算机视觉在精准畜牧业中的可比研究。

Abstract: With the development of precision livestock farming and the advances in computer vision, visual animal biometrics has gained attention. Using biometric technologies that have been proven effective for humans to identify livestock can increase animal welfare as well as production efficiency. However, challenges such as complex scenarios, similar appearances, occlusions, and non-cooperative behavior, as well as the limited amount of publicly available labeled datasets, remain. In this work, we contribute a novel, publicly available cow face benchmark dataset that has been collected in a realistic automatic scenario with 6,838 images of 161 different cows at a dairy farm. In addition to the public dataset, we define two verification and four identification evaluation protocols to foster comparable research in the cow recognition research field. Further, we provide evaluation results on our dataset of six benchmark models, which include models trained on limited data, cross-species fine-tuned models, and zero-shot foundation model approaches.

</details>


### [23] [Alleviating Regional Shortcuts for Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22072)
*Haichen Zhou,Yazhe Lyu,Yixiong Zou,Ruixuan Li,Yuhua Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对小样本增量学习（FSCIL）中新类样本被误分类为基类的问题，发现其根源在于模型对基类判别性区域的过度关注（称为"区域捷径"）。通过组合视角分析，作者提出基于组合学习的方法，学习两个基元集（公共基元集和判别基元集），约束模型同时利用公共基元集进行基类和新类识别，从而缓解区域捷径问题。实验表明该方法在准确率和可解释性上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FSCIL方法倾向于将新类样本误分类为基类，作者发现这是由于模型在基类训练时过度关注最具判别性的区域（基元），形成了"区域捷径"（regional shortcut），导致模型在新类上无法泛化。现有方法缺乏对这一现象的深入分析和理论解释。

Method: 1. 提出组合视角（compositional view）分析基类训练中迁移和复用的空间模式；2. 通过实验和理论分析识别"区域捷径"的存在及其形成机制；3. 提出基于组合学习的方法，学习两个基元集：公共基元集（common set）和判别基元集（discriminative set），通过约束模型学习并利用公共基元集进行基类和新类识别，缓解区域捷径问题。

Result: 在标准FSCIL基准上，该方法在准确率和可解释性上均取得一致性提升，优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文从理论角度解释了FSCIL中新类误分类的根源——区域捷径，并提出了有效的组合学习方法，通过分离公共基元和判别基元来缓解该问题，提升了模型在新类上的泛化能力和可解释性。

Abstract: Few-shot class-incremental learning (FSCIL) aims to incrementally learn novel classes with only a few samples while avoiding forgetting base classes. However, current methods show a tendency to misclassify novel-class samples into base classes, which we find to be caused by the excessive focus on base-class-discriminative regions on novel-class samples. In this work, we aim to explore the underlying mechanism for an interpretation and solution. We first provide a compositional view to analyze the transferred and reused spatial patterns on novel-class samples. Then, through extensive experiments and theoretical analysis, we identify both empirically and theoretically that a shortcut exists in the model's base-class training, which naturally forms the excessive focus on only the most discriminative regions (primitives), which we term as the regional shortcut. Finally, based on this interpretation, to address this problem, we propose a compositional-learning-based method to learn two primitive sets (a common set and a discriminative set), which alleviates the regional shortcut by constraining the model to learn and utilize the common primitive set for base- and novel-class recognition. Extensive experiments on standard FSCIL benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, yielding consistent improvements over existing state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and interpretability.

</details>


### [24] [CommandLM: Data driven behavior level descriptor for ego vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22078)
*Boris Tokic,Constantin Selzer,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CommandLM是一个多模态大语言模型，用于自动驾驶中生成可解释的、人类可读的车辆行为描述，通过融合多传感器数据（LiDAR和多摄像头）并利用Q-Former适配器和LoRA微调的大语言模型，在行为对齐和语言质量上显著优于BLIP-2基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统走向真实部署时，需要可解释的、行为级别的决策机制来保障安全、信任和法规合规性，而现有方法缺乏透明的行为理解能力。

Method: 采用多模态大语言模型架构，通过Q-Former适配器将时间融合的鸟瞰图（BEV）表示（来自LiDAR和多摄像头）连接至经过LoRA微调的量化大语言模型，并在自建CommandLM-nuScenes数据集上训练，生成意图感知、可解释的行为描述。

Result: CIDEr 0.67, BERT-F1 0.88，显著优于BLIP-2基线（CIDEr 0.52, BERT-F1 0.86）；人工评估中58%的描述被评定为准确、高效且符合规则。

Conclusion: 多模态融合与语言推理的结合能够实现高效且透明的自动驾驶行为级理解，CommandLM的可解释输出可作为下游验证系统的有效工具，支持透明的行为审计。

Abstract: As autonomous driving systems move toward real-world deployment, interpretable, behavior-level decision-making is essential for safety, trust, and regulation. We introduce CommandLM, a multimodal large language model that generates concise, human-readable behavior descriptions for ego vehicles from fused multi-sensor data. Our model processes temporally fused bird's-eye view representations from LiDAR and multi-camera inputs via a Q-Former adapter connected to a quantized, LoRA-fine-tuned large language model. Trained on our CommandLM-nuScenes dataset, CommandLM produces intent-aware, interpretable captions suitable for planner supervision and safety auditing. Experiments demonstrate strong linguistic and behavioral alignment, achieving CIDEr 0.67, and BERT-F1 0.88, substantially outperforming the BLIP-2 baseline (CIDEr 0.52, BERT-F1 0.86). In human evaluation, 58% of the generated descriptions were rated accurate, efficient and rule-compliant, confirming their real-world plausibility. While the remaining descriptions may not always select the most efficient, goal-oriented behavior, CommandLM's interpretable outputs enable downstream validation systems to identify and correct such cases, making it an effective tool for transparent behavior auditing. These results show that integrating multimodal fusion with language reasoning yields efficient and transparent behavior-level understanding for autonomous driving. We release our code and dataset at: https://github.com/b-tok/CommandLM

</details>


### [25] [FAIR: Feature-Augmented Implicit Regularization for AI-generated Fake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22087)
*Md Redwanul Haque,Manzur Murshed,Manoranjan Paul,Tsz-Kwan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出特征增强隐式正则化方法FAIR，通过引入场景构成结构（SCS）作为领域不变的宏观结构先验，在训练时约束模型优化轨迹，抑制纹理偏置的捷径学习，推理时丢弃该先验以零开销实现跨生成器AI图像检测的泛化提升，在五个基准上最高提升8.04%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器因训练数据过度拟合低级纹理模式，在未见过的生成器域上泛化失败严重，传统正则化技术施加无差别的参数约束，无法提供跨生成器鲁棒性所需的领域不变结构。

Method: 提出FAIR方法，引入正交的宏观结构先验——场景构成结构（SCS），在训练时对原始特征空间进行特征增强，用领域不变的SCS特征显式惩罚纹理偏置的捷径学习，几何约束模型优化轨迹；推理时完全丢弃该结构先验，实现零架构/计算开销。

Result: 在五个大规模基准上的广泛评估表明，将FAIR集成到现有最先进检测器中显著提升了跨生成器泛化能力，准确率最高提升8.04%，在零样本迁移场景中建立了新的最先进鲁棒性水平。

Conclusion: FAIR通过隐式正则化而非显式参数约束，有效解决了AI生成图像检测的域泛化瓶颈，以零额外开销实现了跨生成器鲁棒性的显著提升。

Abstract: Generalization remains a critical bottleneck in AI-generated image detection. Because many modern generators are proprietary or adversarially modified, existing detectors overfit to the low-level textural patterns of accessible training data, resulting in severe failures on unseen domains. Conventional regularization techniques (e.g., $L_1$/$L_2$ norms, Dropout) apply indiscriminate parametric constraints and fail to provide the domain-invariant structure necessary for cross-generator robustness. To address this, we propose Feature-Augmented Implicit Regularization (FAIR). FAIR introduces an orthogonal, macro-structural prior, specifically, Scene Composition Structure (SCS), during training to geometrically constrain the model's optimization trajectory. By augmenting the primary feature space with domain-invariant SCS features, FAIR explicitly penalizes texture-biased shortcut learning. Crucially, this structural prior is entirely discarded at inference, yielding a smoothed, generalized decision boundary with zero architectural or computational overhead. Extensive evaluations across five massive benchmarks demonstrate that integrating FAIR into state-of-the-art detectors significantly improves cross-generator generalization, boosting accuracy by up to 8.04% and establishing new state-of-the-art robustness in zero-shot transfer scenarios.

</details>


### [26] [Spectral Prior for Reducing Exposure Bias in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22091)
*Yuya Kobayashi,Masato Ishii,Yuhta Takida,Takashi Shibuya,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出光谱对齐（SPA）方法，通过校准扩散模型中间预测的功率谱来解决训练与推理之间的频率依赖性偏差（即暴露偏差），在多种架构上以3-4%的额外计算开销实现一致的生成质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在迭代采样过程中存在误差累积（暴露偏差），且训练与推理之间存在系统性的频率依赖差异，表现为频率依赖的信噪比误差。更重要的是，这种不匹配的方向在不同模型和时间步上各不相同，导致固定的校正规则无法泛化，因此需要一种自适应的校准方法。

Method: SPA方法包含两个阶段：(1) 离线阶段：从训练数据中拟合一个参数化频谱模型作为先验；(2) 推理阶段：通过高效的FFT梯度计算实现频谱引导，在推理过程中校准中间预测的功率谱。该方法计算开销小（仅增加3-4%），且与无分类器引导（CFG）互补。

Result: 在多种架构上均取得了一致的改进，包括像素空间模型（DDPM, ADM）、潜在扩散模型（SD2.0, SDXL）以及流匹配模型（SD3.5, FLUX）。

Conclusion: SPA是一种轻量级、基于引导的频谱校准方法，能有效解决扩散模型中的频率依赖暴露偏差问题，具有架构通用性和低计算开销的优势。

Abstract: Diffusion models typically suffer from error accumulation during iterative sampling, commonly referred to as exposure bias. We reveal systematic frequency-dependent discrepancies between training and inference, which can be interpreted as frequency-dependent SNR error. Crucially, the direction of this mismatch varies across models and timesteps, indicating that fixed correction rules do not generalize. We propose Spectral Alignment (SPA), a lightweight, guidance-based method that calibrates the power spectrum of intermediate predictions to a pre-computed prior. Our approach consists of two stages: (1) offline fitting of a parametric spectrum model from training data, and (2) inference-time guidance via efficient FFT-based gradient computation. SPA introduces minimal computational overhead (3-4\%) and is complementary to Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG). We demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse architectures, from pixel-space models (DDPM, ADM) to latent diffusion models (SD2.0, SDXL) and flow-matching models (SD3.5, FLUX). Our implementation is available at https://github.com/SonyResearch/SPA.

</details>


### [27] [InnoText: A Unified Model for Visual Text Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22101)
*Haowei Liu,Runze He,Jian Lu,Ao Ma,Run Ling,Ke Cao,Jiasong Feng,Wei Feng,Shuo Lu,Yexing Xu,Yun Wang,Jing Wang,Zhanjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InnoText，一个统一的基于DiT的框架，能够同时执行视觉文本生成和编辑任务。通过引入字体大小感知调制模块、小字符增强策略和任务特定区域加权损失，结合高质量双语数据集，在文本生成准确性和编辑质量上达到优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UNet模型难以生成清晰连贯的文本，DiT模型虽表达力更强但通常局限于单一任务，导致训练流程冗余、视觉风格不一致且跨任务泛化能力不足。针对视觉文本任务（尤其是小字体和非拉丁文字如中文）对结构规整性和可读性的高要求，亟需一个统一框架。

Method: 提出InnoText统一框架：(1) Font Size-Aware Modulation (FSAM)模块增强不同字体尺度的表示；(2) Small-Character Aware Augmentation策略提升细粒度保真度；(3) Task-Specific Region Weighted Loss实现自适应优化；(4) 构建高质量中英双语视觉文本数据集。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在生成准确性和编辑质量上均优于现有方法，能生成视觉上吸引人且逼真的文本图像。

Conclusion: InnoText通过统一的DiT框架有效解决了视觉文本生成与编辑的挑战，尤其在小字体和中文场景下表现优越，为多任务视觉文本处理提供了新方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently achieved remarkable success in high-fidelity image synthesis, yet their application to visual text generation and editing remains relatively underexplored. Unlike general image generation, visual text tasks demand precise structural regularity and legibility, which may pose additional challenges for small-scale text and non-Latin scripts such as Chinese. Existing UNet-based models often struggle to produce clear and coherent text, while DiT-based models, though more expressive, are typically limited to a single task, which may lead to redundant training pipelines, inconsistent visual styles, and reduced cross-task generalization. To address these challenges, we propose InnoText, a unified DiT-based framework capable of performing both text generation and editing within a single model. We introduce a Font Size-Aware Modulation (FSAM) module to enhance representations across font scales, a Small-Character Aware Augmentation strategy to improve fine-grained fidelity, and a Task-Specific Region Weighted Loss for adaptive optimization. To support training and evaluation, we also construct a high-quality bilingual (English-Chinese) visual text dataset covering diverse fonts, sizes, and backgrounds. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves superior generation accuracy and editing quality, producing visually appealing and realistic text images.

</details>


### [28] [Projection Pursuit CPCANet for Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22117)
*Yu-Hsi Chen,Abd-Krim Seghouane*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对CPCANet在小批量训练中因样本量不足导致的协方差估计低秩问题，提出PP-CPCANet，通过Stiefel流形上的全局正交基学习与Cayley变换联合优化，以及对称破缺的分离中位数PP散度目标，实现无需协方差矩阵的域泛化方法，在四个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPCANet通过批量公共主成分分析(CPCA)提取域不变结构，但存在小样本量导致的协方差估计秩 deficient 问题，影响域泛化性能。

Method: 1) 提出协方差无关的PP-CPCANet框架，在Stiefel流形上学习全局正交基；2) 通过Cayley变换将正交基与网络参数联合优化；3) 引入对称破缺的分离中位数投影追踪(PP)散度目标，提取公共主成分(CPCs)，提供密集且鲁棒的优化信号。

Result: 在四个域泛化基准数据集上，PP-CPCANet达到SOTA性能，同时保持训练稳定性。

Conclusion: PP-CPCANet通过协方差无关的投影追踪策略和流形优化，有效克服了CPCANet的小样本协方差估计缺陷，为域泛化中的几何对齐方法提供了更稳定且性能更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Domain Generalization (DG) aims to learn representations robust to distribution shifts. Recent geometric alignment methods, such as CPCANet, extract domain-invariant structures through batch-wise Common Principal Component Analysis (CPCA). However, CPCANet suffers from rank-deficient covariance estimation due to the small-sample-size issue in mini-batch training. To address this limitation, we propose Projection Pursuit CPCANet (PP-CPCANet), a covariance-free framework that learns a global orthogonal basis on the Stiefel manifold and jointly optimizes it with network parameters via the Cayley transform. We further introduce a symmetry-breaking detached-median PP dispersion objective to extract common principal components (CPCs) with dense and robust optimization signals. Experiments on four DG benchmarks show that PP-CPCANet achieves SOTA performance while maintaining stable training.

</details>


### [29] [JustDepth: Real-Time Radar-Camera Depth Estimation with Single-Scan LiDAR Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22172)
*Wooyung Yun,Dongwook Kim,Soomok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JustDepth提出一种单阶段雷达-相机深度估计方法，通过固定宽度1D表示、高度融合模块、轻量GNN和训练时置信度解码器，在保持精度前提下将推理速度提升39.7倍，并减少66%的条带伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达-相机深度估计方法多为多阶段流程或依赖辅助标注，导致延迟高、可移植性受限，难以满足自动驾驶对低延迟和高精度的双重需求。

Method: 1）将所有雷达回波聚合成固定宽度的1D表示，使运行时间与点云数量解耦；2）设计高度融合块（Height Fusion Block）融合多模态特征；3）使用轻量图神经网络（GNN）进行全局深度传播；4）引入仅用于训练阶段的置信度解码器，稳定学习且不增加推理开销；5）提出垂直-水平梯度比（VHGR）量化条带伪影，并通过简单数据增强进行抑制。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，与最新方法相比，JustDepth在保持精度的情况下，推理速度提升39.7倍，条带伪影减少66%（以VHGR衡量）。

Conclusion: JustDepth通过单阶段设计和多项创新机制，在雷达-相机深度估计任务上实现了精度、速度和伪影抑制的显著提升，为自动驾驶感知提供高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate yet low-latency depth is essential for radar-camera perception in autonomous systems. Cameras provide rich appearance but lack metric scale, whereas automotive radar offers metric range but is sparse and noisy. Many pipelines are multi-stage or depend on auxiliary annotations, increasing latency and limiting portability. We introduce JustDepth, a single-stage radar-camera depth estimator trained only with radar, camera, and single-scan LiDAR. All radar returns are aggregated into a fixed-width 1D representation, decoupling runtime from point count. A Height Fusion Block fuses modalities, a lightweight GNN propagates depth globally, and a training-only confidence decoder stabilizes learning with zero test-time cost. We mitigate stripe artifacts via simple augmentations and quantify them using the Vertical-Horizontal Gradient Ratio (VHGR). On nuScenes, compared to recent state-of-the-art methods, JustDepth maintains accuracy while reducing inference time by 39.7x and stripe artifacts by 66% as measured by VHGR.

</details>


### [30] [A Framework for Individual Tree Growth Reconstruction Using Multi-Platform Laser Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22129)
*Daniella Tavi,Valtteri Soininen,Lassi Ruoppa,Jesse Muhojoki,Juha Hyyppä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合深度学习分割、多平台激光扫描数据（ALS/MLS/TLS）和高度生长缩放模型的框架，用于估算北方森林单木的胸径（DBH）和材积生长量。利用2014-2025年间采集的136个点云数据，结果表明该框架的生长估计与人工测量高度一致，且时间稳健性良好，误差在5-6年后趋于稳定。该方法无需多次林下扫描即可实现可靠的树木生长监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 北方森林中树木生长重建面临历史茎级数据稀缺、旧传感器误差传播以及生长量量级与测量不确定性相近等挑战，亟需一种可靠、稳健的单木生长估计框架。

Method: 使用深度学习分割从MLS点云中 delineate 树木，并迁移至其他点云实现多时相对应；从MLS/TLS数据提取树干曲线，ALS数据用于树高估计；基于高度生长的缩放模型重建不同时间的茎干属性并估计生长量。

Result: 模型估计的生长量与人工测量高度一致；5年和10年生长的RMSE：DBH为55-111%和26-67%，材积为31-87%和21-67%；误差在5-6年后稳定，12年后最大RMSE：DBH为8-12%，材积为12-23%。

Conclusion: 结合MLS/TLS茎干测量与多时相ALS树高数据，提供了一种无需多次林下扫描的稳健单木生长估计框架。

Abstract: Accurate tree-level forest monitoring using laser scanning data requires reliable tree delineation, consistent tree correspondence across multitemporal point clouds, and accurate estimation of tree attributes and their change. Reconstructing tree growth in boreal forests is challenging due to the scarcity of historical stem-level data, propagation of errors from older sensors into change estimation, and growth rates with a magnitude of measurement uncertainty. This study investigates a framework for estimating individual tree diameter at breast height (DBH) and stem volume growth using 136 point clouds acquired between 2014--2025 with 11 scanners on airborne (ALS), mobile (MLS), and terrestrial laser scanning (TLS) platforms across boreal forest test sites. Trees were delineated from an MLS point cloud using deep learning-based segmentation which was transferred to the remaining point clouds, resulting in reliable multitemporal tree correspondence. Stem curves were derived from MLS/TLS data, with ALS data used for height estimation, enabling DBH and volume estimation and time series. A height growth-based scaling model was used to reconstruct stem attributes across time and estimate growth. Results showed that modeled growth achieved higher agreement with manual growth estimates than differencing independently estimated attributes from point clouds. The modeled-manual 5- and 10-year growth RMSEs were 55--111\% and 26--67\% for DBH, and 31--87\% and 21--67\% for volume, respectively, depending on plot difficulty. The scaling model was temporally robust, with errors remaining stable or stabilizing after 5--6 years, reaching maximum RMSEs of 8--12\% for DBH and 12--23\% for volume after 12 years. Combining MLS/TLS-derived stem measurements with multitemporal ALS-derived heights provided a robust framework for individual tree growth estimation without requiring multiple under-canopy scans.

</details>


### [31] [Geometric 2D Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22325)
*Christoph Jahn,Urs Waldmann,Bastian Goldluecke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需语义数据、能在小样本条件下构建装配关系场景图的方法，利用Faster R-CNN提取几何特征，经Transformer生成邻接矩阵，再通过基于注意力图卷积网络（aGCN）的孪生网络进行消息传递，以表征零部件间的装配关系，并在玩具模型数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费品生产和机器人装配中，理解零部件之间的装配关系至关重要，但现有方法往往依赖大量语义数据，难以在数据稀缺场景下应用。因此，作者希望开发一种不依赖语义信息且能处理小数据集的装配关系建模方法。

Method: 该方法首先使用Faster R-CNN模型检测零部件并生成几何表示，然后通过Transformer架构处理这些几何表示以生成邻接矩阵，最后将邻接矩阵输入基于注意力图卷积网络（aGCN）的孪生网络，通过消息传递机制来表征零部件之间的连接关系，从而构建场景图。

Result: 在由可组装成运输车辆的玩具模型零部件构成的研究数据集上进行了验证，结果表明该方法能够有效构建和表征零部件间的装配关系场景图。

Conclusion: 提出的方法不依赖语义数据且能处理小样本数据，通过几何表示与图神经网络的结合，成功实现了装配关系的场景图构建，为机器人装配和工业生产中的装配理解提供了一种可行的技术方案。

Abstract: In production processes for consumer products, assembly instructions are essential not only for planning but also for executing the production process. Likewise in robotics, it is crucial for an assembly robot to understand how components fit together and can be assembled. To facilitate these tasks, we contribute a method for constructing scene graphs to represent and characterize assembly relationships between components. Our approach does not rely on semantic data and is capable of handling a very small dataset. To realize this, the output of a Faster R-CNN model is used to create geometric representations, which are then processed by a transformer architecture to generate an adjacency matrix. This matrix serves as input to a Siamese network that uses message passing based on an attentional graph convolutional network (aGCN) architecture to characterize the connections between the components. We validate our method on a study dataset of toy model components which can be assembled into transportation vehicles.

</details>


### [32] [GLI-AL: A Multi-Modal Glioma MRI Label Resource with Unified Anatomy-Lesion Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22135)
*Xingyu Xiang,Shuang Hao,Fan Wang,Jianhua Ma,Chunfeng Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对BraTS-GLI数据集在联合分割中未标注白质高信号（WMH）导致标签噪声的问题，提出了BraTS-GLI Anatomy-Lesion数据集。该资源基于BraTS 2023-GLI训练队列，提供了1,251套统一八类解剖-病灶标签，包含394例纯化子集和857例扩展子集。实验表明，WMH感知的监督方法能在保持健康组织分割性能的同时，提高对共存病灶的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BraTS-GLI数据集专注于胶质瘤亚区域分割，未系统标注共存的白质高信号（WMH）。在联合分割任务中，这些未标注的异常区域会被视为正常组织，引入任务特定的标签噪声，影响分割模型的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 基于BraTS 2023-GLI训练队列构建的受控访问标签衍生资源，提供1,251套统一八类解剖-病灶标签（包括116例需修复图像输入的病例）。数据分为394例纯化子集和857例扩展子集，附带元数据涵盖标签来源、图像修复需求、质量控制状态等。使用MedNeXt和T1/FLAIR输入进行验证研究。

Result: 相比原始BraTS-GLI注释，该资源通过将健康脑组织和先前未标记的共存异常纳入统一标签空间，大幅扩展了前景监督。验证实验表明，WMH感知的监督在保持域内GLI和外部WMH数据集的健康组织分割性能的同时，相比噪声对照训练提高了对共存病灶的敏感性。

Conclusion: 该资源支持联合解剖-病灶监督、标签噪声分析和可重复评估，旨在促进脑肿瘤分割研究中对共存病理异常的系统性处理。

Abstract: Existing BraTS-GLI datasets provide a widely used benchmark for adult glioma MRI segmentation, but their task definition focuses on tumor subregions and does not systematically represent coexisting white matter hyperintensities (WMH). In joint segmentation settings, such unlabeled abnormalities introduce task-specific label noise by treating pathological regions as normal tissue. To address this limitation, we introduce BraTS-GLI Anatomy-Lesion, a controlled-access, labels-only derived resource built from the BraTS 2023-GLI training cohort. The resource provides 1,251 unified eight-class anatomy-lesion label sets aligned with the original four-modal MRI cases, including image-repair labels for 116 cases requiring repaired imaging inputs. The cohort is organized into a 394-case purified subset and an 857-case extended subset, with case-level metadata covering label source, image-repair requirements, quality-control status, access conditions, checksums, and release boundaries. Compared with the original BraTS-GLI annotations, the resource substantially expands foreground supervision by incorporating healthy brain tissues and previously unlabeled coexisting abnormalities within a unified label space. A validation study using MedNeXt and T1/FLAIR inputs suggests that WMH-aware supervision preserves healthy-tissue segmentation performance across both in-domain GLI and external WMH datasets, while improving sensitivity to coexisting lesions relative to noisy-control training. The resource is intended for scientific research and supports joint anatomy-lesion supervision, label-noise analysis, and reproducible evaluation. Data are available at https://www.synapse.org/Synapse:syn75210889/wiki/, and code is available at https://github.com/xyx200/brats-gli-anatomy-lesion-code. The data resource DOI is https://doi.org/10.7303/SYN75210889.

</details>


### [33] [SM4RT: Learning Structured Motion Geometry for 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22534)
*Shing Ho J. Lin,Wenzhao Zheng,Dong Zhuo,Yuqi Wu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SM4RT是一种用于端到端3D重建和结构化运动感知的4D重建Transformer，通过将场景运动分解为SE(3)中的6D扭转序列（运动基）并利用稀疏的逐像素分配权重恢复密集场景运动，使同一物体上的点共享共同的刚体运动轨迹，从而保持物理运动的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动感知方法将运动视为独立的逐点位移，忽略了物理运动的结构化本质；而真实物体遵循刚体运动学，点应集体运动而非孤立运动。

Method: 提出Structure-of-Motion表示，将场景运动分解为紧凑的运动基（每个基为SE(3)中6D扭转的时间序列），通过稀疏、时间共享的逐像素分配权重恢复密集场景运动；设计并行运动几何编码器和解码器，从单目RGB视频中单次前向传播联合推断3D几何、世界坐标运动及场景运动学结构。

Result: SM4RT在保持场景运动几何结构的同时，实现了强大的运动重建性能。

Conclusion: 将物理运动的结构化先验（刚体运动学、SE(3)变换）引入4D重建框架，能够有效提升动态场景理解的质量和几何一致性。

Abstract: Geometry Foundation Models (GFMs) have substantially advanced monocular 3D reconstruction, yet extending this capability to 4D dynamic understanding remains a fundamental challenge. Most existing motion perception methods (e.g., sparse tracking, dense point-wise flow) treat motion as independent point-wise displacements, ignoring the structured nature of physical motion. However, real-world objects usually obey rigid-body kinematics, and points thus usually move collectively, not in isolation. Motion itself possesses geometric structure: physical objects undergo a set of rigid-body transformations governed by SE(3), rather than unstructured point-wise displacements. Building on this insight, we propose SM4RT, a Structured Motion 4D Reconstruction Transformer for end-to-end 3D reconstruction and structured motion perception. SM4RT introduces Structure-of-Motion to represent scene dynamics, where scene motion is decomposed into a compact set of motion bases, each represented as a temporal sequence of 6D twists in SE(3). Dense scene motion is then recovered by sparse, time-shared per-pixel assignment weights over these bases, ensuring points on the same object share a common rigid-body motion trajectory. SM4RT introduces a parallel motion geometry encoder and decoder that jointly infer 3D geometry, world-coordinate motion, and scene kinematic structure in a single forward pass from monocular RGB video. SM4RT achieves strong motion reconstruction performance while preserving the geometric structure of scene motion.

</details>

Abstract: Accurate pixel-level classification of coronary angiograms is critical for cardiovascular disease assessment, yet the field lacks standardized evaluation protocols. In this work we demonstrate a new benchmark for the assessment of deep learning models which densely classify pixels of coronary angiograms to one of SYNTAX classes (or background). The evaluation covers 24 distinct architectures starting with classic convnets to recent state-space-based vision algorithms. We release CARDIAG - a multi-center, multi-label dataset which we carefully split to reliably compute metrics, accounting for diameter error, overlap, centerline quality and calibration. The data contains SYNTAX labels, binary, uncertainty and segmentation masks as well as intermediate frames together with the selected non-sensitive DICOM metadata. From the multitude of algorithms, we nominate ConvNeXt V2 encoder with DeepLab V3 Plus decoder as the best performing, achieving macro $F_1=0.456$, which we then ensemble with Mamba U-Net and Feature Pyramid Network, for an increased $F_1=0.479$. We demonstrate all the architectures to be well calibrated and determine the generalization of the top 5 methods, together with the data efficiency of these architectures. We highlight the importance of both high-resolution and low-resolution features in encoding. We also demonstrate the model correctness in the context of patient demographic, vessel sides and projection angle configurations. Overall the released benchmark allows for future studies to robustly and rigorously assess the proposals, not only for SYNTAX segmentation, but lesion detection and many more.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星类地形存在低纹理、感知混叠、恶劣光照以及因前向运动和无约束行驶方向导致的稀疏弱重叠视角，现有图像到图像与图像到地图匹配管线性能严重下降，亟需一种不依赖传统特征匹配的鲁棒重定位方法。

Method: 提出基于3DGS可微分地图的视觉重定位方法，核心是几何感知训练策略：首次将MVS与LiDAR深度结合提供几何监督，联合优化光度损失与几何损失，使3DGS模型更拟合底层场景几何，进而实现单图像6-DoF位姿直接估计。

Result: 在行星模拟环境数据上的大量实验验证了该方法有效性，在严苛条件下重定位精度相比现有方法有显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何感知联合优化策略能生成几何与光度一致性更好的3DGS模型，从而在极端视觉条件下实现更鲁棒、更精确的单图像重定位。代码已开源。

Abstract: Visual localization becomes extremely challenging in planetary-like terrains characterized by low texture, perceptual aliasing, harsh illumination, and sparse, weakly overlapping viewpoints induced by forward rover motion and unconstrained driving directions. Under these conditions, state-of-the-art image-to-image and image-to-map matching pipelines suffer significant performance degradation. In this work, we propose a visual relocalization method that departs from classical correspondence-based pipelines by directly estimating camera poses against a differentiable map representation built with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Our key contribution is a geometry-aware training strategy that combines photometric and geometric losses, where the geometric supervision is provided for the first time by combining multi-view stereo (MVS) and LiDAR depths. We show that this joint optimization produces a 3DGS model that better fits the underlying scene geometry, leading to improved photometric and geometric consistency and more robust, accurate single-image 6-DoF pose estimation. Extensive experiments on data acquired in planetary-analog environments validate the effectiveness of our approach, showing substantial gains in relocalization accuracy under challenging conditions. Code is available at https://github.com/DLR-RM/multimodal-gsplat-relocalization.

</details>


### [36] [dRAE: Representation Autoencoder with Hyper-Spherical Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22148)
*Tianren Ma,Lin Long,Chuyan Chen,Mu Zhang,Junbo Zhao,Tong Zhang,Qixiang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出超球面量化（HSQ）方法，通过角路由机制解耦语义内容与特征幅度，解决了高维视觉表示离散化中的码本坍缩问题，实现了可扩展的高保真离散表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法在高维视觉表示离散化时面临码本坍缩问题，无法在保持语义一致性的同时进行扩展。作者识别出根本原因是欧几里得码本目标与表示空间的各向异性几何结构不匹配，导致码本嵌入具有高方差幅度尺度和不均匀的角度分布，阻碍了可扩展性。

Method: 提出超球面量化（HSQ），通过角路由机制将语义内容与特征幅度解耦，防止码分配被尺度而非语义所主导。基于此构建离散表示自编码器（dRAE），实现高保真重建同时保持语义完整性并支持可扩展的码本预算。

Result: 在码本大小扩展至131,072时仍能保持一致的性能提升，实现了100%的码本利用率，简化了训练流程，并在理解和生成任务上均表现出强性能。

Conclusion: HSQ有效地解决了视觉表示离散化中的码本坍缩问题，通过解耦语义与幅度的角路由策略，实现了大规模、高利用率、语义一致的离散表示学习，为连接视觉与语言模型提供了可行的桥梁。

Abstract: In this work, we aim to discretize the high-dimensional visual representations to bridge the gap with language models - a non-trivial challenge, as existing quantization methods suffer from codebook collapse, failing to scale while preserving semantic coherence. We identify the root cause as metric mismatch: standard Euclidean codebook objectives are fundamentally misaligned with the anisotropic geometry of representation space, leading to codebook embeddings with high-variance magnitude scales and uneven angular distributions that hinder scalability. To address this, we propose Hyper-Spherical Quantization (HSQ), which decouples semantic content from feature magnitude via angular routing, preventing code assignment from being dominated by scale rather than meaning. The resulting discrete Representation Autoencoder (dRAE) achieves high-fidelity reconstruction while preserving semantic integrity and supporting scalable codebook budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent performance gains as the vocabulary size scales to 131{,}072, along with 100\% codebook utilization, simplified training pipeline, and strong performance across understanding and generation tasks.

</details>
