Abstract: Bowel obstruction is a common and potentially life-threatening gastrointestinal condition. In the face of rising diagnostic workloads, the automated diagnosis of bowel obstruction on CT scans supports radiologists by accelerating detection and improving patient outcomes. In this work, we propose a deep learning framework with a multi-task objective that jointly detects bowel obstruction and localizes its transition zone. Additionally, we extend the method with an inherently interpretable classification method that locates the suspected transition point within a slice. It does so by learning a probabilistic selection mask that faithfully bases the classifier's prediction solely on a small image region. The proposed method is evaluated on an internal dataset comprising 1,427 abdominal CTs. Here, the model achieves an obstruction detection test accuracy of 93% and a Hit@10 transition zone localization of 95%. As the first method to reliably localize the transition zone, this marks a significant step towards the automated identification of this critical clinical landmark.

### [38] [LayoutLite: Token-Level Implicit Layout Analysis for Efficient Document OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22200)
*Xudong Liu,Bicheng Wan,Yulin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LayoutLite是一个轻量级即插即用模块，通过隐式词元级布局分析，在保持OCR识别质量的同时大幅减少视觉词元数量，从而加速基于视觉语言模型的文档OCR系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端OCR系统（基于VLM）在复杂文档场景中性能优异，但文档图像产生大量视觉词元（包括空白边缘和视觉冗余区域），导致推理效率低下。直接使用通用视觉词元压缩方法可能丢失对OCR至关重要的细粒度细节。

Method: LayoutLite在视觉编码器和语言解码器之间执行隐式词元级布局分析：1) 聚合视觉编码器的多层表示；2) 使用轻量级评分网络预测每个视觉词元的重要性得分；3) 移除低信息词元并保留原始空间位置信息。训练采用强化学习框架（GRPO目标优化OCR输出一致性）配合辅助布局监督信号，无需人工标注。

Result: 在OmniDocBench上显著减少视觉词元长度和推理成本，识别质量几乎无损。在FireRed-OCR和Logics-Parsing-V2上，50%词元压缩率下保持几乎相同的评分，同时prefill延迟、FLOPs和KV缓存内存减少超过40%，仅增加少量推理开销。

Conclusion: 词元级隐式布局分析是一种有效且实用的加速VLM-based OCR系统的方法，LayoutLite在效率与质量之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: End-to-end OCR systems based on vision-language models have achieved strong performance in complex document OCR, but their efficiency is limited by the large number of visual tokens produced from document images. Many of these tokens correspond to blank margins or visually redundant regions, yet directly applying generic visual token compression methods may remove OCR-critical fine-grained details. In this paper, we propose LayoutLite, a lightweight plug-and-play module for efficient document OCR. Instead of relying on explicit document layout detection, LayoutLite performs implicit layout analysis at the token level between the vision encoder and the language decoder. It aggregates multi-layer visual representations from the vision encoder, and predicts an importance score for each visual token with a lightweight scoring network. Low-information tokens are then removed before entering the language decoder while preserving the original spatial positional information of retained tokens. To train LayoutLite without human annotations, we cast token selection as a reinforcement learning problem and optimize it with a group-relative policy optimization objective driven by OCR output consistency, together with an auxiliary layout supervision signal to stabilize training. Experiments on OmniDocBench demonstrate that LayoutLite can substantially reduce visual token length and inference cost with negligible degradation in recognition quality. We further evaluate LayoutLite on two OCR-specialized VLMs, FireRed-OCR and Logics-Parsing-V2. Under up to 50% token compression, LayoutLite preserves almost the same score on both models while reducing prefill latency, FLOPs, and KV cache memory by over 40%, with only a small additional inference overhead. These results show that token-level implicit layout analysis is an effective and practical approach for accelerating VLM-based OCR systems.

</details>


### [39] [Filling Before Advancing: Capability-Gap-Driven Post-Training for Scenario-Specialized Remote Sensing MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22205)
*Yuheng Zong,Minghua Wang,Xin Zhao,Zhi-Hui Zhan,Antonio Plaza,Jon Atli Benediktsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FBA（填充再推进）方法，通过能力缺口驱动的后训练策略解决遥感多模态大语言模型（RS-MLLM）在细粒度场景专业化中的不足。以海岸港口场景为例，构建CPRS三层监督数据集和有序三阶段训练（RS语义锚定、域桥收敛、证据驱动场景微调），并建立HarborEval八轨诊断基准。实验表明，FBA在多个模型上显著优于直接SFT和坍缩SFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RS-MLLM在通用航拍图像理解上有所提升，但地球观测应用需要细粒度的场景专业化能力，面临高质量场景数据稀缺和能力覆盖不完整的双重挑战。为填补这一能力缺口，本文提出FBA方法。

Method: 将场景适应问题形式化为能力缺口驱动的后训练问题，提出FBA框架。具体实例化为海岸港口场景理解，构建CPRS三层监督数据集并设计三阶段有序训练：1）RS语义锚定（俯视视觉-语言对齐）；2）域桥收敛（目标场景与桥接场景共享RS先验）；3）证据驱动场景微调。同时构建HarborEval八轨诊断基准。

Result: 在可比训练预算下，LLaVA-v1.5上HarborEval得分从57.95（直接SFT）提升至70.29（FBA）；Qwen3-VL上从81.09提升至83.37。FBA优于坍缩SFT，并在港口相关的VRSBench/RSVQA子集和OpenEval上领先。

Conclusion: FBA方法通过渐进式能力缺口填充和分阶段特定角色训练，有效提升了RS-MLLM在细粒度场景上的专业化性能，验证了多阶段后训练策略的有效性。

Abstract: Remote sensing multimodal large language models (RS-MLLMs) have improved general aerial-image understanding. However, Earth observation applications require fine-grained scenario specialization, constrained by scarce high-quality scenario data and incomplete capability coverage. We formulate this adaptation as a capability-gap-driven post-training problem and propose filling before advancing (FBA). Rather than relying on single-stage supervised fine-tuning (SFT) over target-domain samples, FBA first fills prerequisite capability gaps before advancing toward scenario specialization. We instantiate FBA for coastal harbor understanding, a representative multi-source scenario, by constructing CPRS (Coastal-Port Remote Sensing), a three-layer supervision dataset coupled with three ordered stages: (1) RS semantic anchoring for overhead-view visual-language alignment; (2) domain-bridge convergence for shared RS priors across target and bridging scenarios under different modalities; and (3) evidence-grounded scenario tuning for downstream performance. We construct HarborEval, an eight-track diagnostic benchmark covering perception, spatial understanding, robustness, and generation. Under comparable training budgets, HarborEval increases from 57.95 with Direct-SFT to 70.29 with FBA on LLaVA-v1.5, and from 81.09 to 83.37 on Qwen3-VL. FBA also outperforms Collapsed-SFT and leads on harbor-related VRSBench/RSVQA subsets and OpenEval. Stage-wise and role-replacement analyses validate progressive gap filling and stage-specific roles. Public examples and release updates for CPRS, HarborEval, code, and trained weights are available at https://github.com/Z0ngL1ng/filling-before-advancing.

</details>


### [40] [Deep Convolutional Large-Margin $\ell_p$-SVDD for Visual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22212)
*Alireza Dastmalchi Saei,Shervin Rahimzadeh Arashloo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DLM-SVDD，一种深度融合大间隔新颖性检测框架，通过联合学习卷积特征和显式核基决策边界，结合ℓₚ-SVDD的大间隔优化与深度表示学习，在异常样本稀缺且类别极度不平衡的场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核方法能产生几何上合理的决策区域但基于固定特征，深度检测器可学习任务特定表示却缺乏显式的间隔感知核边界，两者优势未能结合。

Method: 提出DLM-SVDD框架：1）联合学习卷积特征与显式核决策边界；2）基于ℓₚ-SVDD进行显式间隔最大化和非线性松弛惩罚；3）提出交替优化方案——Frank-Wolfe更新凸对偶边界 + CNN更新基于边界恢复的平滑间隔违反损失；4）分析不同核近似策略的效率-精度权衡以提升可扩展性。

Result: 多个标准基准实验表明，该方法在性能上持续优于基线，与最先进方法相比整体表现强劲，且在严重不平衡类别分布下联合表示-边界学习方案依然有效。

Conclusion: DLM-SVDD成功融合了深度表示学习与大间隔核边界优化，为视觉异常检测提供了一种既能自适应学习特征又具备显式几何决策边界的有效范式。

Abstract: Visual anomaly detection requires adaptive representations and reliable decision boundaries, particularly when anomalous training samples are scarce and class distributions are highly imbalanced. Classical kernel-based methods yield principled geometric decision regions but typically operate on fixed features, while deep detectors learn task-specific representations but often fail to provide an explicit margin-aware kernel boundary. In this study, we propose DLM-SVDD, a deep large-margin novelty-detection framework that jointly learns convolutional features and an explicit kernel-based decision boundary. By drawing on the large-margin $\ell_p$-Support Vector Data Description ($\ell_p$-SVDD) approach, the proposed method performs explicit margin maximization and nonlinear slack penalization while adapting the representation to the target task. To train the proposed model, we present an optimization scheme that alternates between a Frank--Wolfe--based update of the convex dual boundary and a CNN update step operating on a smooth margin-violation loss induced by the recovered boundary. To improve scalability, we analyze the efficiency--accuracy trade-offs for different kernel approximation strategies, deriving practical propositions for large-scale anomaly detection.
  Extensive experiments on multiple standard benchmarks show consistent performance improvements over the baseline and strong overall performance compared with state-of-the-art methods while illustrating that the proposed joint representation--boundary learning scheme remains effective under severe imbalanced class distributions.

</details>


### [41] [TRaM-VSR: Importance-Aware Token Routing and Merging for One-Step Diffusion Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22231)
*Sicheng Gao,Zhuyun Zhou,Yixuan Liu,Tong Shen,Zongwei Wu,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRaM-VSR提出了一种Token路由与合并框架，通过融合运动敏感的时间线索和语义文本相似度来估计token重要性，并利用离线规划器校准重要性以指导分组网络块中的路由，实现高保真局部流与紧凑全局流的自适应token分配，在显著加速视频超分辨率推理的同时保持最先进的重建质量和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大规模扩散Transformer先验的VSR方法虽感知质量优异，但因处理密集时空token序列的二次计算成本而难以实用，现有高效方法又存在不可逆细节丢失和时间闪烁问题，尤其在一步扩散模型中更为突出。

Method: 提出Token路由与合并框架TRaM-VSR：1) 融合运动敏感时间线索与语义文本相似度估计token重要性，隔离动态对象与结构边界；2) 通过离线规划器进一步校准和调整重要性，指导最优分组的网络块中的路由；3) 在每个路由组内，结构关键token由高保真局部流处理，信息较少的token聚合成紧凑全局流，两者均通过网络深度调制并与扩散模型的多粒度特性对齐。

Result: 实验表明TRaM-VSR显著加速推理，同时保持最先进的重建质量和鲁棒的时间一致性。

Conclusion: TRaM-VSR通过自适应token分配有效解决了扩散模型VSR的效率与质量权衡问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video super-resolution (VSR) using large-scale Diffusion Transformer (DiT) priors achieves exceptional perceptual quality but is often impractical due to the quadratic computational cost of processing dense spatio-temporal token sequences. Existing efficiency-oriented methods risk irreversible detail loss and temporal flickering, a vulnerability especially pronounced in one-step diffusion models. To address this, we propose TRaM-VSR, a Token Routing and Merging framework for adaptive token allocation, leveraging both context-aware video priors and network-level priors. First, token importance is estimated by fusing motion-sensitive temporal cues with semantic text similarity, isolating dynamic objects and structural boundaries. Next, this importance is further calibrated and adjusted by an offline planner to guide routing across optimally grouped network blocks. Technically, within each routed group, structurally critical tokens are processed in a high-fidelity local stream, while less informative tokens are aggregated into a compact global stream, both modulated by network depth and aligned with the multigranular nature of diffusion models. Extensive experiments show that TRaM-VSR accelerates inference significantly while preserving state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and robust temporal consistency. The code is available at https://github.com/Ree1s/TRaM-VSR.

</details>


### [42] [AgentHOI: Multi-Agent Reasoning for Human-Object-Interaction Video Generation via Implicit Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22241)
*Ziyao Huang,Shunkai Li,Juan Cao,Chenyu Li,Youliang Zhang,Zixiang Zhou,Cong Wang,Yuan Zhou,Qinglin Lu,Fan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentHOI提出了一种“先思考后生成”的文本驱动人-物交互视频生成框架，通过多智能体推理（感知、交互、运动规划）将高层级文本意图转化为物理执行，并利用隐式文本-运动对齐策略将文本到运动的先验知识蒸馏到视频扩散模型中，无需显式运动输入即可生成自然、保物的交互视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人-物交互（HOI）视频生成方法严重依赖显式运动控制，限制了跨不同物体和交互的可扩展性与泛化能力。

Method: 提出AgentHOI框架，采用“先思考后生成”（thinking-before-generation）范式，通过多智能体（感知、交互、运动规划）推理桥接文本意图与物理执行；引入隐式文本-运动对齐策略，将文本到运动的先验知识蒸馏到视频扩散模型中，无需显式运动输入。

Result: 实验表明，AgentHOI在交互自然性、物体外观保持度以及复杂文本指令遵从性方面显著提升，尤其在穿戴、骑乘等以物体为中心的场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: AgentHOI通过多智能体推理和隐式文本-运动对齐，有效解决了文本驱动HOI视频生成中缺乏显式运动控制时的交互合理性问题，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models have spurred interest in human-object interaction (HOI) video generation, which demands fine-grained control over interaction logic beyond single-subject animation. However, existing HOI methods rely heavily on explicit motion control, limiting scalability and generalization across diverse objects and interactions. In this study, we propose AgentHOI, a text-driven HOI video generation following a thinking-before-generation framework that bridges the gap between high-level textual intent and physical execution through multi-agent reasoning over perception, interaction, and motion planning. Building upon the generated interaction plans, we further strengthen text-driven motion understanding. We introduce an implicit text-motion alignment strategy that distills text-to-motion priors into the video diffusion model, enabling robust HOI synthesis without explicit motion inputs at inference. Experiments show that AgentHOI significantly improves interaction naturalness, object appearance preservation, and adherence to complex textual instructions across challenging object-centric scenarios such as wearing and riding. The code is available at https://github.com/bone-11/agenthoi.

</details>


### [43] [From level set evolution to threshold optimization: A grayscale level set framework for image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22255)
*Xingkai Li,Jiebao Sun,Fanghui Song,Zhichang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种快速分割框架，通过去除传统水平集方法中的长度正则化项，将PDE演化转化为一维阈值搜索，显著加速了退化图像（强噪声、灰度不均）的分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水平集方法中的长度正则化项导致数值不稳定和高计算成本，亟需一种更快速、稳定的退化图像分割方法。

Method: 理论证明长度项影响$|\nabla φ|=1$性质；定义光滑图像类；构建灰度水平集；将PDE演化转化为一维阈值搜索。

Result: 在多种退化图像（强噪声、灰度不均）上验证了分割性能，计算速度在大尺度图像上具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 证明了在特定光滑约束下长度项非必要，所提框架在保证分割精度的同时大幅提升计算效率。

Abstract: The segmentation of multiple degradations has been a challenging problem in the field of image segmentation. Existing level set approaches commonly adopt a length regularization term to constrain the geometric shape of the segmentation contour. However, the introduction of the length term often results in numerical instability and high computational cost. In this paper, we show that the length term is not essential under certain smoothness constraints, and theoretically prove that the presence of the length term affects the property of $|\nabla φ|=1$. Based on the finding, we define a class of smooth images, construct the grayscale level set, and propose a fast segmentation framework for degraded images, such as heavily noisy images and intensity inhomogeneous images. The framework transforms PDE evolution into one-dimensional threshold search, which has significant advantages in computational speed, especially on large-scale images. Experiments validate the segmentation performance of the proposed framework on various degraded images.

</details>


### [44] [RadSight: Towards Perceptually Reliable Multimodal Radiology Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22293)
*Jianqin Liu,Weiwei Cao,Wanxing Chang,Ruifeng Yuan,Bowen Shi,Zhilin Zheng,Xianjie Zhang,Ling Zhang,Peng Wang,Jianpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Perception-Bench基准（113万样本，6个维度），揭示现有医学多模态大模型在低级视觉感知（如病灶位置、大小、密度）存在根本性缺陷，进而提出基于双2D/3D编码器架构的感知驱动模型RadSight，通过四阶段渐进式学习（视觉-语言对齐→细粒度视觉感知→临床诊断→诊断解释）在8.37M语料上训练，在全部6个维度及公开基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学MLLMs虽然在临床任务上表现提升，但经常出现视觉理解错误，其诊断不可靠的根源被认为是低层视觉感知这一关键瓶颈被忽视。

Method: 构建Perception-Bench基准（113万样本，涵盖属性判断、空间定位、空间理解、疾病预测、异常检测、报告生成6个维度，2D和3D影像）；提出RadSight模型，采用双2D/3D编码器架构保留原始空间结构，通过四阶段渐进式学习（视觉-语言对齐→细粒度视觉感知→临床诊断→诊断解释）在837万感知导向语料上训练。

Result: RadSight在Perception-Bench全部6个维度上一致优于现有MLLMs，在空间定位和临床诊断上提升尤为显著；同时在公开2D和3D医学基准上也取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 稳健的低层视觉感知是可靠临床理解的关键基础，Perception-Bench可用于系统追踪医学MLLMs的视觉理解失败根源。

Abstract: Medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected to perform complex image understanding tasks, yet their reliability is often compromised by frequent errors in visual interpretation. To systematically trace these failures, we traverse the hierarchy from high-level clinical tasks down to fundamental visual perception. We therefore introduce Perception-Bench, a large-scale benchmark comprising 1.13 million samples that assesses medical MLLMs across six dimensions: attribute judgment, spatial grounding, spatial understanding, disease prediction, anomaly detection, and report generation, spanning both 2D and 3D radiology images. Our analysis on Perception-Bench reveals that existing MLLMs lack the ability to capture even the most basic lesion attributes, such as location, size, and density. This inability to ground clinical outputs in primary visual evidence reveals that the models' diagnostic unreliability is rooted in a critical but overlooked bottleneck in low-level visual perception. Motivated by this, we propose RadSight, a perception-driven MLLM built upon a dual 2D/3D encoder architecture that preserves native imaging spatial structures. RadSight formulates medical image understanding as a four-stage progressive process: visual-language alignment, fine-grained visual perception, clinical diagnosis, and diagnostic interpretation. The model is trained on an 8.37 million perception-oriented corpus using progressive curriculum learning. On Perception-Bench, RadSight consistently outperforms existing MLLMs across all six evaluation dimensions, with particularly strong gains in spatial grounding and clinical diagnosis. It also achieves consistent improvements on public 2D and 3D medical benchmarks, further demonstrating that robust low-level visual perception is a critical foundation for reliable clinical understanding. Code and model will be publicly available.

</details>


### [45] [fMRI2Face: A Full-HD fMRI-Video Dataset and Geometry-Guided Neural Decoding Framework for Dynamic Human Face Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22302)
*Jingyang Huo,Xiangru Huang,Chentao Shen,Yikai Wang,Yun Wang,Jianxiong Gao,Shihao Jin,Yanwei Fu,Jianfeng Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个与可控全高清数字人面部视频配对的fMRI数据集fMRI-Face，并基于该数据集提出了fMRI2Face几何引导的神经视频解码框架，能够从大脑活动重建高保真动态面部视频，在身份保留、面部几何和运动一致性方面优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fMRI面部解码受限于缺乏高分辨率、可控的神经数据集，且现有方法难以同时恢复身份特定外观和时变面部动态，因此需要构建结构化数据集和高效解码方法。

Method: 构建fMRI-Face数据集（62,856对fMRI-视频样本）；提出fMRI2Face框架，包含脑源表观上下文（捕获全局身份特征）和可变形3D面部控制（提供几何感知的姿态/表情引导），通过神经控制视频扩散与潜在补全实现重建。

Result: fMRI2Face在重建保真度、身份保留、面部几何和运动一致性上均优于代表性神经解码基线。

Conclusion: fMRI-Face和fMRI2Face建立了研究动态面部感知的可控平台，为基于fMRI的数字人重建提供了新基准。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic human faces from brain activity provides a powerful way to study how the mind perceives identity, expression, and facial motion. However, progress in fMRI-based face decoding has been limited by scarce controlled, high-resolution neural datasets and by methods that struggle to recover both identity-specific appearance and time-varying facial dynamics. We present fMRI-Face, the first fMRI dataset paired with controllable full-HD digital human facial videos rendered at 1920$\times$1080 resolution. During scanning, participants watched photorealistic, background-free facial videos with controlled identity, expression, and head pose, while fMRI activity was recorded. The resulting dataset contains 62,856 paired fMRI-video samples, providing a structured resource for studying dynamic face perception and reconstruction. Building on this dataset, we propose fMRI2Face, a geometry-guided neural video decoding framework for reconstructing facial videos from fMRI signals. fMRI2Face derives two complementary neural controls from brain activity: Brain-derived Appearance Context, which captures global identity-related visual attributes, and Morphable 3D Facial Control, which provides explicit geometry-aware guidance for pose, expression, and non-rigid facial dynamics. These controls are integrated through Neural-Controlled Video Diffusion with auxiliary latent completion, enabling high-fidelity facial video reconstruction directly from brain activity. Experiments show that fMRI2Face consistently improves reconstruction fidelity, identity preservation, facial geometry, and motion consistency over representative neural decoding baselines. Together, fMRI-Face and fMRI2Face establish a controlled platform for studying dynamic face perception and provide a new benchmark for fMRI-based digital human reconstruction.

</details>


### [46] [SLIP: Segmentation with Low-latency Interactive Prompting for 3D Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22332)
*Baptiste Podvin,Alexandre Ancel,Flavio Milana,Chiara Innocenzi,Davide Arrigo,Federico Espinola Schulze,Guido Torzilli,Jacques Marescaux,Daniel George,Alexandre Hostettler,Toby Collins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLIP是一个端到端可训练的交互式3D医学图像分割框架，通过解耦图像编码与提示引导精化、引入轻量级补丁记忆库，实现低延迟、支持可逆提示的一次性交互分割，在13个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能，并通过受控前瞻性用户研究验证了优于现有方法（如nnInteractive）的用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于补丁的交互式分割方法（如nnInteractive）存在高交互延迟、对连续交互响应有限、不支持可逆提示等问题，且评估主要依赖模拟而非真实用户交互研究，缺乏充分的人体验证。

Method: SLIP框架将图像编码与提示引导精化解耦：图像特征一次性计算并复用；轻量级补丁记忆库维护跨补丁的交互感知分割状态，通过传播交互上下文实现预测更新，支持可逆提示且无需重算图像特征，显著降低交互延迟。该框架兼容多种图像编码器，可训练单一模型用于跨解剖结构和成像模态的通用交互分割。

Result: 在13个公开数据集上达到SOTA交互分割性能；相比现有方法具有更低的交互延迟、更高的响应性、支持可逆提示；通过受控前瞻性用户研究（3项临床标注任务、6名专家参与者及主观可用性指标）获得更高用户偏好。

Conclusion: SLIP通过解耦设计与补丁记忆库有效解决了现有交互式分割方法的延迟和响应性问题，在客观指标和主观用户体验上均优于nnInteractive等方法，填补了该领域缺乏真实用户验证的空白。

Abstract: Interactive deep image segmentation enables efficient medical image annotation by iteratively refining predictions from user prompts, such as positive and negative clicks. Recent patch-based methods, including nnInteractive, achieve strong segmentation performance but remain limited in annotation workflows by high interaction latency, limited responsiveness to successive interactions, and the lack of support for reversible prompting. Furthermore, evaluation relies predominantly on simulated rather than controlled real-user interaction studies. We present SLIP, an end-to-end trainable framework for interactive 3D medical image segmentation that decouples image encoding from prompt-guided refinement. Image features are computed once and reused, while a lightweight patch memory bank maintains an interaction-aware segmentation state shared across patches. This representation enables prediction updates by propagating interaction context throughout the image, supports reversible prompting without recomputing image features, and substantially reduces interaction latency. By separating image representation from interactive reasoning, SLIP remains compatible with a wide range of image encoders. We train a single SLIP model for general interactive segmentation across diverse anatomical structures and imaging modalities. Beyond standard simulated evaluation, we conduct a controlled prospective user study comparing manual segmentation, nnInteractive, and SLIP across three clinical annotation tasks, six expert participants, and subjective usability measures, addressing the limited human validation of interactive segmentation methods. SLIP achieves SOTA interactive segmentation performance across 13 public datasets while providing lower interaction latency, greater responsiveness, support for reversible prompting, and higher user preference than existing approaches.

</details>


### [47] [SiPhy: Single-Image Physical Property Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22355)
*Hoang Le,Joonwoo Kwon,Elkhan Ismayilzada,Yufei Zhang,Zijun Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SiPhy是一个能从单张RGB图像推断物体物理属性（质量、刚度、弹性等）的统一框架，通过结合3D视觉线索、深度信息与语言先验知识，在多个基准上达到SOTA，远超多视图重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理属性推理方法大多依赖多视图重建或基于物理的监督信号，难以从单张图像直接推断质量、刚度、弹性等物理属性，限制了在仿真和具身AI中的实际应用。

Method: SiPhy包含以下关键技术：1) 从单张RGB图像采样伪体素点并提取CLIP特征；2) 利用VLM提出候选材料类别并对齐视觉特征；3) 基于部件的对比聚合器确保区域一致性；4) 轻重感知精细化模块提升稠密物体的厚度与体积估计。

Result: 在ABO-500、MVImgNet-100和PhysXNet-100上达到单图像SOTA，质量MnRE相比PUGS提升93%，密度MAE相比NeRF2Physics降低35.5%，杨氏模量误差降低23.5%。在真实手-物交互数据集上也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: SiPhy证明结合视觉-语言先验可以从单张图像有效推理物理属性，且可作为单视图物理理解的数据标注引擎。

Abstract: Inferring physical properties such as mass, stiffness, and elasticity from a single image is essential for simulation and embodied AI, yet most existing approaches rely on multi-view reconstruction or physics-based supervision. We introduce SiPhy, a unified framework for single-image physical property reasoning that aligns 3D-aware visual cues, depth with language-based material knowledge. From one RGB image, SiPhy samples pseudo-voxel points, extracts CLIP features, and grounds them to material candidates proposed by a VLM. A part-based contrastive aggregator enforces region consistency, while a heaviness-aware refinement improves thickness and volume estimation for dense objects. Across ABO-500, MVImgNet-100, and PhysXNet-100, SiPhy achieves state-of-the-art single-image performance, surpassing multi-view reconstruction methods by improving mass MnRE by up to 93% (vs. PUGS), reducing density MAE by 35.5% (vs. NeRF2Physics), and lowering Young's modulus error by 23.5%. We further validate SiPhy on real hand-object interaction datasets, demonstrating its potential as a data annotation engine for physical understanding from single-view imagery.

</details>


### [48] [Active few-shot segmentation by reinforcing data selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22371)
*Chenlan Zhao,Benny Wong,Timothy F. Lundberg,Ahmed M. Elsayed,Abdallah Aljarkas,Hamad A. Aljamaan,Lynn Karam,Qianye Yang,Yipeng Hu,Claire C. Villette,Shaheer U. Saeed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的支持集选择框架，用于小样本医学图像分割，通过联合优化支持集而非独立评分样本，在盆腔MRI数据集上优于随机选择和现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动数据选择方法主要独立地对样本进行评分，没有明确考虑样本之间的互补性和交互作用，导致支持集适配效果不佳。

Method: 采用强化学习框架，将支持集选择建模为序列决策问题，智能体直接从无标签候选池中预测能最大化下游分割性能的整个支持集，实现联合优化。

Result: 在跨机构盆腔MRI数据集上，该方法优于随机选择和当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 支持集的互补性对模型有效适配至关重要，强化学习是优化适配集的可行且有效的方法。

Abstract: Few-shot learning enables medical image segmentation models to adapt to new tasks using only a small number of labelled examples. However, adaptation performance depends strongly on which examples are selected for the support set. Effective support sets should capture relevant variation within the target domain and be informative for adaptation, with constituent samples providing complementary information. Despite this, existing active data selection approaches largely prioritise samples individually and do not explicitly account for interactions between examples. In this work, we propose a reinforcement learning framework for support-set selection in few-shot medical image segmentation, enabling support sets to be optimised jointly rather than through independent sample scoring. Given a pool of unlabelled candidate images, an agent directly predicts a support set that maximises downstream segmentation performance. Experiments on a cross-institutional pelvic MRI dataset demonstrate improvements over random selection and current state-of-the-art methods. Our findings highlight the importance of support-set complementarity for effective adaptation and demonstrate the potential of reinforcement learning for optimising adaptation sets.

</details>


### [49] [IR275K: A Benchmark for Infrared Multi-Frame Super-Resolution Toward Efficient Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22380)
*Jie Deng,Heyang Wang,Changxin Wang,Junkai Shen,Hongyi Chen,Zhiping He,Hongxing Qi,Xudong Zhang,Jianyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IR275K是一个包含594个红外视频序列、共275,196帧的多帧超分辨率基准数据集，并提出了轻量级状态空间模型CGMamba，在X4超分辨率下达到33.19dB PSNR，验证了空间锚定对红外条件下SSM跨帧门控的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外遥感中多帧超分辨率评估因依赖私有数据集和临时协议而碎片化，现有基准未能捕捉红外视频特有的热对比度、传感器噪声、弱纹理和平台引起的帧间变化，缺乏统一、可复现的评估基准。

Method: 构建IR275K基准数据集（594个序列，275,196帧），提供序列级训练/验证/测试划分和可复现的X4评估协议；提出CGMamba轻量级状态空间模型（10.90M参数，112.14G FLOPs），结合2D旋转位置编码（2D RoPE）和中心引导的跨Mamba（CGCM）融合进行隐式多帧重建。

Result: CGMamba在红外X4超分辨率下达到33.19dB PSNR，以更低计算成本超越红外单图像超分辨率基线0.35-0.52dB；消融实验表明去除2D RoPE导致1.53dB下降并出现严重网格伪影。

Conclusion: IR275K为红外MFSR方法的精度-效率评估提供了可复现的基础；显式空间锚定（2D RoPE）对于稳定红外条件下SSM跨帧门控至关重要，为资源受限红外感知中具有空间感知的SSM设计提供了起点。

Abstract: Efficient processing is becoming increasingly important in infrared remote sensing, where satellite constellations produce large volumes of observations under constrained detector resolution, power, and downlink bandwidth. Multi-frame super-resolution (MFSR) offers a software-based route to spatial enhancement, but its evaluation in infrared sensing remains fragmented across private datasets and ad-hoc protocols. Existing benchmarks do not explicitly capture the thermal contrast, sensor noise, weak texture, and platform-induced frame-to-frame variation that characterize infrared video. We introduce IR275K, a curated benchmark containing 594 infrared video sequences and 275,196 frames. It provides sequence-level train/validation/test splits and a reproducible X4 evaluation protocol. As an initial architectural probe, we further evaluate CGMamba, a lightweight state-space model with 10.90M parameters and 112.14G FLOPs. CGMamba combines 2D rotary position encoding (2D~RoPE) with center-guided cross-Mamba (CGCM) fusion for implicit multi-frame reconstruction. It achieves 33.19dB PSNR, outperforming infrared single-image super-resolution references by 0.35--0.52~dB at substantially lower computational cost. Ablation results show that removing 2D~RoPE from CGCM causes a 1.53dB drop and severe grid-like artifacts. This indicates that explicit spatial anchoring is critical for stabilizing SSM-based cross-frame gating under infrared conditions. IR275K provides a reproducible foundation for accuracy--efficiency evaluation of infrared MFSR methods, while the architectural analysis offers a concrete starting point for spatially aware SSM design under resource-constrained infrared sensing. Dataset and evaluation resources are available at: https://github.com/InfraRecon7/IR275K.

</details>


### [50] [Correlation-Aware and Gaussianity-Preserving Robust Latent Angular Watermarking for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22386)
*Yebin Zheng,Haonan An,Guang Hua,Zhiping Lin,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出潜空间角度水印(LAW)方法，通过将水印比特编码为潜变量对之间的反平行角度(±π/2)来保持高斯性，并推导出解码角度误差方差与潜对范数成反比。进一步提出基于幅度的变体LAW-M，在几何最稳定的潜维度嵌入水印。理论上严格表征了水印后潜变量的自相关结构，证明相关性仅限于稀疏的离对角元素且值为固定±π/4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜空间水印方法存在两个关键问题：1）破坏潜变量的高斯性，导致水印易受检测或移除攻击；2）破坏潜变量的i.i.d.条件，引起相关性退化并导致生成保真度损失。这些问题尚未被严格刻画。本文旨在设计一种既能保持潜变量高斯性又能最小化相关性退化的水印方法。

Method: 受各向同性高斯旋转不变性启发，提出潜空间角度水印(LAW)：将水印比特编码为潜变量不相交对之间的反平行角度(±π/2)，并保持高斯分布。基于角度误差方差与潜对范数平方成反比的理论推导，进一步提出LAW-M变体，将水印锚定在几何最稳定的高范数潜维度上。

Result: 理论结果表明：1）解码角度误差方差 var(Δφ) ∝ 1/ρ²；2）水印后潜变量的自相关结构得到严格闭式刻画，相关性仅限于稀疏离对角元素且值为固定±π/4。LAW-M通过锚定高范数维度获得额外鲁棒性增益。

Conclusion: LAW方法通过保持潜变量高斯性和最小化相关性退化，在抗水印检测/移除攻击和生成保真度之间取得了平衡，为扩散模型潜空间水印提供了理论严谨的解决方案。

Abstract: Latent domain watermarking for diffusion models embeds watermarks directly into the latent prior, enjoying non-intrusiveness to model parameters and seamless integration with the generation process. However, due to the violation of latent Gaussianity or sensitivity to normal and malicious perturbations during latent inversion, existing methods are prone to watermark detection or removal attacks. A further overlooked problem is the violation of the i.i.d. latent condition after watermarking, which leads to latent correlation degradation and generation fidelity loss. Although this has been externally measured by FID, the internal correlation structure has yet to be rigorously characterized. To address the above issues, and motivated by the rotation-invariant property of isotropic Gaussian, we propose \textit{Latent Angular Watermarking (LAW)}, which encodes watermark bits as antipodal angles ($\pmπ/2$ relative to a reference pair) between disjoint pairs of latent elements while preserving the Gaussianity. The antipodal ($π$-separation) encoding maximizes geometric separation between bit values, and we prove that the decoding angular-error variance is proportional to the norm of the latent pair, i.e., $\operatorname{var}(Δφ) \propto 1/ρ^2$. We further propose a magnitude-driven variant, LAW-M, which anchors watermark bits in the most geometrically stable latent dimensions, yielding additional robustness gains. Theoretically, we provide a rigorous characterization of the induced correlation degradation, deriving in closed form the autocorrelation structure of the watermarked latent and proving that correlations are confined to a sparse, structured set of off-diagonal elements with fixed $\pmπ/4$ values.

</details>


### [51] [Deformable Triangle Splatting: Flexible Primitives for Real-Time Radiance Field Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22446)
*Oriol Jiménez-Ayguadé,Antonio Agudo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出可变形三角形泼溅（Deformable Triangle Splatting），通过在三角形每条边上引入K个可学习控制点（标量位移），使原本凸边界的2D基元能够表示非凸、弯曲和凹面结构，并设计了基于重心坐标的可微分光栅化管线，利用绕数测试和可学习窗口函数实现平滑透明度过渡。在真实场景中该方法在视觉质量和通用性上优于现有非体积基元方法，且保持高效渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于2D基元的辐射场方法（如高斯盘、三角形）仅支持凸边界，导致弯曲和凹面结构需要大量冗余基元才能表示，效率低下且质量受限。

Method: 1) 为每个三角形每条边引入K个控制点，每个控制点由单个可学习标量位移参数化，使边界可向内或向外偏移，实现非凸形状表示；2) 在三角形重心坐标空间中设计可微分光栅化管线，使用绕数测试判断像素是否在变形后的基元内部；3) 通过两个可学习参数（锐度和角平滑度）的窗口函数及每个基元的标量不透明度，实现从内部到边界的平滑透明度过渡。

Result: 在多种真实场景中验证，该方法在视觉质量和通用性上优于最近的基于非体积基元的方法，同时保持有竞争力的渲染效率。

Conclusion: 可变形三角形泼溅通过引入边缘控制点位移和非凸形状表示能力，在保持高效光栅化渲染的同时，显著提升了2D基元对复杂几何结构的建模能力，为辐射场方法提供了一种更灵活的场景表示方式。

Abstract: Recent radiance field methods represent scenes with 2D primitives that offer surface alignment and efficient rasterization, from Gaussian disks to triangles, yet all rely on convex boundaries: curved and concave structures demand excessive primitives. We introduce Deformable Triangle Splatting, which augments each triangle with $K$ control points per edge, each parameterized by a single learnable scalar displacement that shifts the boundary inward or outward, enabling non-convex shape representation while preserving the three base vertices that define the 3D plane. To render these non-convex primitives differentiably, we design a rasterization pipeline in the triangle's barycentric coordinate space, ensuring view-consistent rendering. A winding number test determines whether each pixel lies inside the deformed primitive, and a window function controlled by two learnable parameters, sharpness and corner smoothness, together with a per-primitive scalar opacity, produces the smooth opacity transition from interior to boundary. Validation is done in a variety of real-world scenes, outperforming recent works based on non-volumetric primitives in terms of visual quality and versatility while still achieving competitive rendering efficiency.

</details>


### [52] [Optimal Transport Image Representation and Deep Covariance Alignment (CORAL) for Control Valve Stiction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22486)
*Seshu K. Damarla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结合最优传输(OT)成像技术与深度相关对齐(Deep CORAL)算法，将控制器输出和过程变量转换为二维OT图像，通过CNN提取域不变特征，在20个工业基准回路上实现90%准确率和100%召回率的阀杆粘滞检测方法，有效缓解了仿真数据与真实工业数据之间的域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制阀粘滞是工业过程中导致振荡和控制性能下降的常见原因，现有数据驱动方法在仿真数据上训练后难以泛化到真实工业环境，主要障碍是仿真数据与真实数据之间存在域偏移(domain shift)。

Method: 1) 将控制器输出(OP)和过程变量(PV)的闭环信号转换为二维最优传输(OT)图像，刻画控制回路的动态行为；2) CNN编码器通过联合优化交叉熵损失(基于有标签仿真数据)和Deep CORAL协方差对齐损失(对齐仿真数据与无标签目标域工业数据)来学习域不变表征；3) 在学到的域不变特征上训练下游分类器进行粘滞检测。

Result: 在20个工业基准回路上：正确诊断18个回路，对13个粘滞案例实现100%召回率，准确率90.00%，F1-score 92.86%。相比基于手工特征的传统方法，显著缓解了域偏移问题。

Conclusion: 结合OT成像与Deep CORAL领域自适应的方法能够有效弥合仿真与真实工业数据之间的差距，为工业控制回路提供鲁棒且高可靠性的阀杆粘滞检测方案。

Abstract: Control valve stiction is a common cause of unwanted oscillations and poor control-loop performance in industrial processes. Data-driven methods can automatically detect stiction, but models trained purely on simulated data often struggle to generalize to real industrial control loops due to domain shift. To bridge this gap, this work propose a novel stiction detection methodology that combines optimal transport (OT) imaging technique with deep correlation alignment (Deep CORAL) algorithm. Closed loop signals: controller output and process variable are converted into two-dimensional OT images. These images capture the dynamic behaviour of control loops. The proposed methodology includes a convolutional neural network (CNN) encoder (or feature extractor) trained to learn domain-invariant representations by optimizing a combined objective: a cross-entropy loss on labeled simulation data and a Deep CORAL (covariance-alignment) loss between simulation data and unlabeled target-domain industrial data. Downstream classifiers trained on the domain-invariant target features were evaluated on an independent test set of 20 benchmark loops from industrial stiction data benchmark. The proposed methodology successfully diagnosed 18 out of the 20 loops and achieved100% recall across all 13 stiction cases, an accuracy of 90.00% and an F1-score of 92.86%. Compared to standard baseline approach (hand-crafted features-based method), the proposed methodology significantly mitigates domain shift, providing robust, highly reliable stiction detection for real-world industrial control loops.

</details>


### [53] [Twins: Learn to Predict Unified Representations with Focal Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22531)
*Kaixiong Gong,Xin Cai,Bin Lin,Hao Wang,Yunlong Lin,Mingzhe Zheng,Bohao Li,Jian-Wei Zhang,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Twins统一连续令牌空间，通过通道级联ViT和VAE特征解决多模态理解与图像生成中表征不匹配问题，并设计焦点回归目标缓解扩散Transformer中的优化不平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在连续空间中通常依赖两种不同的表征（语义特征用于理解、低级潜变量用于生成），导致潜在空间不匹配，阻碍了统一表征的构建。

Method: 提出Twins，在同一令牌网格上通道级联ViT和VAE特征形成统一连续令牌空间，保持序列长度不变；针对扩散Transformer中ViT与VAE组件的优化不平衡问题，采用焦点回归目标用于流匹配，对VAE中大误差维度进行加权。

Result: 在ImageNet上，相比朴素MSE损失，无分类器引导下gFID提升最高10.57；在多模态理解基准上表现有竞争力，且提升了重建保真度。

Conclusion: 缩小了面向理解与面向生成的表征之间的差距，验证了统一连续令牌空间在多模态理解与生成联合建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models seek a shared visual token space that supports both multimodal understanding and image generation. Discrete methods unify the interface via a shared codebook, whereas continuous pipelines often rely on two disparate representations -- semantic features (e.g., ViT) for understanding and low-level latents (e.g., VAE) for synthesis -- resulting in mismatched latent spaces. We propose Twins, a unified continuous token space formed by channel-wise concatenating ViT and VAE features on the same token grid, so the sequence length is unchanged and attention cost does not increase. However, jointly modeling Twins in a Diffusion Transformer exposes a severe optimization imbalance: the model fits the ViT component well but struggles to match the VAE latent distribution. We trace this imbalance to three sources of heterogeneity: frequency bias, intrinsic dimensionality, and condition-aligned vs condition-independent uncertainty. To address it, we adapt a focal regression objective for flow matching that upweights large-error VAE dimensions, better balancing optimization across the ViT and VAE components. On ImageNet, this yields up to 10.57 gFID gain over naive MSE loss without classifier-free guidance. Twins also performs competitively on multimodal understanding benchmarks and improves reconstruction fidelity, narrowing the gap between understanding- and generation-oriented representations.

</details>

### [54] [FlowEvo: Self-Evolving Agents through the Co-Evolution of Workflows and Executable Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21596)
*Zeyu Ren,Ling Yue,Ran Li,Yishu Wang,Shengxiang Xu,Hanmo Liu,Shaowu Pan,Shimin Di*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEvo是一个无需训练的框架，通过将成功执行轨迹编译为可复用的技能记录（skill record）并存入技能库，在推理时实现工作流到技能、技能到工作流的反馈循环，从而在不更新模型参数的情况下持续积累和优化任务解决能力。在ALFWorld、HumanEval和GSM8K等基准测试中，FlowEvo在准确率和成本之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在执行过程中发现的实用流程往往是暂态的，仅能解决当前任务，无法以系统化的形式被保留下来以惠及未来任务，导致重复劳动和效率低下。

Method: FlowEvo包含三个核心机制：(1) 工作流到技能编译（workflow-to-skill compilation）：从成功执行轨迹中提取可复用的可执行构件；(2) 技能到工作流反馈（skill-to-workflow feedback）：通过直接执行或结构化上下文注入的方式，检索已积累的技能以支持未来问题解决；(3) 技能管理（skill curation）：监控下游效用，抑制导致负迁移的技能。技能记录经过接口检查、重放检查和安全检查后存入技能库。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达到82.8%的成功率，比最强基线高出23.6个百分点，且每轮平均token用量不到最高效基线的一半。在HumanEval和GSM8K上也表现出最佳准确率-成本权衡。消融实验验证了每个机制的有效性。

Conclusion: FlowEvo通过工作流-技能-工作流的反馈循环，使智能体能够在不更新模型参数的情况下持续积累和精炼任务解决能力，显著提升效率与性能。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly solve complex tasks by constructing inference-time workflows that combine reasoning, tool use, and code execution. While such workflows enable flexible problem solving, the useful procedures discovered during execution are often transient: they help solve the current task but are not retained in a form that can systematically benefit future tasks. We present FlowEvo, a training-free framework that compiles successful traces into reusable skill records. Each record pairs a callable artifact with auxiliary structured guidance, and admission applies interface, replay, and safety checks where feasible. These skill records persist in a skill bank at inference time. FlowEvo is organized around three coupled mechanisms: (1)~workflow-to-skill compilation, which extracts reusable executable artifacts from successful traces; (2)~skill-to-workflow feedback, which retrieves accumulated skills to support future problem solving through either direct execution or structured context injection; and (3)~skill curation, which monitors downstream utility and suppresses skills that cause negative transfer. Through this workflow--skill--workflow feedback loop, FlowEvo enables agents to accumulate and refine task-solving capability over time without updating model parameters. Experiments on benchmarks spanning interactive environments (ALFWorld) and code/math generation (HumanEval, GSM8K) show that FlowEvo achieves the best accuracy-cost tradeoff among the evaluated baselines under our implementation settings. On ALFWorld, FlowEvo achieves an 82.8\% success rate, 23.6 percentage points above the strongest baseline, while its average token usage per episode is less than half that of the most efficient baseline. Controlled ablations confirm that each mechanism contributes to the overall result. The code is public at https://github.com/DEFENSE-SEU/FlowEvo.

</details>


### [55] [Risk Is Not the Target: A Monotonic Framework for Evaluating Wildfire Operational Risk Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21597)
*Nicolas Caron,Christophe Guyeux,Maxime Coulmeau,Benjamin Aynes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的单调评估框架来衡量 wildfire 风险系统的运行一致性（而非预测准确性），并在法国阿尔卑斯-滨海省比较了三种方法（DFE、GRU、FARS），发现DFE尽管分类指标差但单调性最优，GRU局部单调性好但风险分布不均匀，FARS继承上游信号缺陷。核心结论是：好的风险模型应能解释运营动态，而非准确预测火灾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器学习指标（如F1-score、IoU）评估火灾风险系统存在根本性缺陷：它们衡量的是事件预测准确性，而非风险信号在运营层面的连贯性。实际应用中，风险评分需要与运营负载（火灾数量、干预时间、资源部署）单调一致，因此需要新的评估方法。

Method: 提出单调评估框架，核心思想是测量风险评分增加时运营负载是否单调递增。在法国阿尔卑斯-滨海省比较三种方法：基于专家知识的DFE指数、基于GRU的预测模型、以及结合预测AI与LLM推理的混合多智能体系统FARS。

Result: DFE虽分类指标差，但在全风险尺度上单调行为最均衡；GRU模型局部单调性强但风险水平分布不均；FARS继承并暴露了上游信号的结构性缺陷而未能修正。

Conclusion: 好的风险模型不应以准确预测火灾为目标，而应使其序数尺度能有效解释运营动态。这一范式转换是本文的核心贡献。

Abstract: Evaluating wildfire risk systems using standard machine-learning metrics such as F1-score or IoU is fundamentally flawed: these metrics assess event prediction accuracy, not the operational coherence of a continuous risk signal. This work proposes a novel monotonic evaluation framework that measures whether increases in a predicted risk score consistently correspond to increases in observed operational load, such as number of fires, intervention time, and deployed resources. Moreover, we compare three structurally different approaches on the French Alpes-Maritimes department: the expert-based DFE index, GRU- based predictive models, and FARS, a hybrid multi-agent system combining predictive AI with LLM-based reasoning. Experimental results reveal that the DFE, despite poor classification metrics, exhibits the most balanced monotonic behavior across the full risk scale. GRU models achieve strong local monotonicity but fail to produce well-distributed risk levels. FARS inherits and reveals the structural limitations of upstream signals rather than correcting them. The central finding is a paradigm shift: a good risk model does not predict fires accurately, but one whose ordinal scale meaningfully explains operational dynamics, as proved in this paper. Code of the monotonic framework is available on github.

</details>


### [56] [Wavelet Phase Diffusion for Structurally and Semantically Consistent Sim-to-Real Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21628)
*Kaiwen Wang,Frank Bieder,Yinzhe Shen,Carlos Fernandez,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Omer Sahin Tas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wavelet Phase Diffusion方法，通过在Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform域进行局部化相位注入，并采用低频随机化（LFR）技术，实现了无需配对数据、无额外推理开销的仿真到真实图像翻译，在结构保持、语义一致性和计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真到现实图像翻译方法存在不足：基于条件的方法引入昂贵控制模块，配对数据方法依赖复杂合成流程且可能改变场景几何和语义，无训练编辑方法缺乏学习到的外观先验导致感知质量有限，而相位保持扩散虽具潜力但傅里叶域全局谱耦合导致空间伪影（如振铃和边界泄漏），破坏结构和语义一致性。

Method: 提出Wavelet Phase Diffusion，包含两个核心组件：(1) 在Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform域操作，利用其局部化小波包实现空间自适应相位注入，避免全局谱干扰；(2) 低频随机化（LFR）替换低频小波包，使模型脱离合成光照先验，获得分布内的真实世界外观。两者均使用非配对开放域数据训练，推理开销可忽略不计，且空间局部性支持实例级翻译。

Result: 在vKITTI→KITTI图像翻译中，该方法在真实感和语义一致性上超越先前方法，同时保持有竞争力的结构对齐。在CARLA视频翻译中，该方法接近配对数据方法的真实感，同时将VLM规划器的ADE和FDE分别降低5.4%和5.1%。

Conclusion: Wavelet Phase Diffusion通过小波域局部化相位注入和低频随机化，在无需配对数据、无额外推理开销的条件下，有效解决了仿真到现实翻译中的外观差距问题，实现了高质量的结构保持、语义一致性和实例级翻译能力。

Abstract: Simulation-to-reality translation must bridge the appearance gap between synthetic and real domains while preserving structural and semantic consistency. Conditioning-based methods achieve spatial alignment but introduce computationally expensive control modules. Paired-data methods achieve realism but rely on complex synthesis pipelines, often altering scene geometry and semantics. Training-free editing methods avoid both constraints but lack a learned appearance prior, limiting their perceptual quality. Recently proposed phase-preserving diffusion presents a promising alternative, but Fourier-domain formulations are constrained by global spectral coupling. This coupling induces spatial artifacts such as ringing and boundary leakage, thereby degrading structural and semantic consistency. We introduce Wavelet Phase Diffusion, which addresses this through two components. First, we operate in the Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform domain, whose localized wavelet packets enable spatially adaptive phase injection without global spectral interference. Second, Low-Frequency Randomization (LFR) replaces the low-frequency packet, decoupling the model from the synthetic illumination prior and enabling in-distribution real-world appearance. Both components train on unpaired open-domain data, and introduce negligible inference overhead. The spatial locality further enables instance-level translation, where individual objects or regions are translated to photorealistic appearance independently while the surrounding scene remains untranslated. On vKITTI $\to$ KITTI image translation, ours outperforms prior methods in realism and semantic consistency while maintaining competitive structural alignment. For CARLA video translation, ours approaches the realism of paired-data methods while reducing VLM planner ADE and FDE by $5.4\%$ and $5.1\%$, respectively.

</details>


### [57] [Securing Multimodal AI through Internal Information Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21600)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Hyeonjeong Ha,Qiusi Zhan,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowGuard是一种轻量级推理时防御框架，通过监控多模态大语言模型中的跨模态一致性来检测有害输入。该方法利用Partial Information Decomposition导出的FlowVectors量化跨模态冗余、协同和模态特定主导性，在仅使用良性数据训练的一类分类问题中，将攻击成功率从>90%降至<15%，效用损失<3%，延迟降低6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型存在单模态系统所没有的攻击面： adversaries可以将恶意意图跨模态分布以规避单模态安全措施。现有防御方法检查原始输入或输出，忽略了内部融合过程，导致脆弱且计算开销大。

Method: FlowGuard提出FlowVectors，基于Partial Information Decomposition量化跨模态冗余、协同和模态特定主导性，通过监控融合后的多模态预测是否与单模态语义证据一致来检测有害输入。采用一类分类问题，仅使用良性数据训练。

Result: 在未见过的攻击上，攻击成功率从>90%降至<15%，效用损失<3%，延迟降低高达6倍。

Conclusion: 监控跨模态一致性为多模态推理提供了一种高效且有效的防御方法。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models introduce attack surfaces absent in unimodal systems: adversaries can distribute malicious intent across modalities to evade unimodal safeguards. This motivates using cross-modal consistency as a detection signal rather than inspecting each modality in isolation. Our key observation is that benign inputs induce compatible predictive behavior from text-only and vision-only reasoning that stabilizes when fused, whereas adversarial manipulation disrupts this consistency, causing abnormal multimodal behavior. Existing defenses that examine raw inputs or outputs overlook this internal fusion process, rendering them brittle and computationally expensive. We propose FlowGuard, a lightweight inference-time framework that detects harmful inputs by monitoring internal multimodal consistency. Unlike approaches that rely on scalar confidence metrics, FlowGuard derives FlowVectors inspired by Partial Information Decomposition that quantify cross-modal redundancy, synergy, and modality-specific dominance, capturing whether fused multimodal predictions remain aligned with unimodal semantic evidence. In a one-class classification problem trained solely on benign data, FlowGuard reduces Attack Success Rates from >90% to <15% on unseen attacks, with <3% utility loss and up to a 6 times latency reduction. Our results demonstrate that monitoring cross-modal consistency offers an efficient and effective defense for multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [58] [From Frame-Level Recognition to Event-Level Confirmation: Repair Traces and Runtime Failure Analysis of Public-Space Gesture Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21601)
*M. Meng,Yansong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了公共空间手势交互系统中"识别到交互的鸿沟"问题，基于景区交互式自助终端的8份工程维修记录，提取了20个失败实例并归纳为6类工作故障，提出了事件级运行时抽象机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手势交互研究多聚焦于帧级识别准确率，但实际部署系统中用户关心的是意图动作是否能转化为稳定的交互事件，存在识别到交互的鸿沟。

Method: 分析了8份景区交互式自助终端工程维修记录，涵盖4种手势任务，提取20个失败实例，归纳为非互斥的6类工作故障，并组织修复机制形成事件级运行时抽象。

Result: 识别出6类工作故障：模型输出退化、时间错配、几何尺度不稳定、坐标渲染失配、运行时生命周期故障、反馈同步与恢复故障。提出了手势模型与交互任务之间的事件确认运行时抽象。

Conclusion: 贡献在于：基于部署的故障分类法、事件确认运行时抽象和案例研究发现，不声称提出了新的识别模型、大规模用户评估或量化准确率提升。

Abstract: Public-space gesture interaction is often evaluated as a frame-level recognition problem, but deployed systems expose a different failure boundary. In scenic kiosks, exhibition halls, and service terminals, users experience whether an intended action becomes a stable interaction event, not whether individual hand-landmark frames are correct. We call this the recognition-to-interaction gap.
  This paper analyzes 8 engineering repair records from a scenic-area interactive kiosk project, covering 4 gesture tasks: two-hand bowing, single-hand fist shaking, two-hand catching control, and knowledge-graph node hovering. From these traces, we extract 20 failure instances and organize them into six non-exclusive working failure classes: model-output degeneration, temporal mismatch, geometric-scale instability, coordinate-rendering mismatch, runtime lifecycle failure, and feedback synchronization and recovery failure.
  We further organize recurring repair mechanisms into an event-level runtime abstraction between the hand-landmark model and the interaction task. The contribution is deliberately bounded: a deployment-grounded failure taxonomy, an event-confirmation runtime abstraction, and case-study findings. We do not claim a new recognition model, large-scale user evaluation, or quantified accuracy gains.

</details>


### [59] [Transferable Latency Prediction for Fast LLM Screening on Heterogeneous Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21602)
*Xiaolong Tu,Vinod K. Mishra,Venkat R. Dasari,Anu G. Bourgeois,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种运行时感知的延迟预测框架，用于在异构边缘设备上部署大语言模型。该框架将推理请求建模为硬件-运行时-模型-提示配置，分离预填充和解码阶段，通过门控预测模型融合静态描述符与动态硬件遥测。在Pixel设备上验证，总延迟R²从0.953提升至0.960，解码延迟R²从0.957提升至0.973；跨设备迁移通过轻量校准可将R²从负值恢复至0.94以上。实验表明延迟高度依赖设备和运行时环境，该框架可降低性能分析成本并支持延迟感知的LLM部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构边缘设备上部署LLM时，推理延迟受模型架构、提示行为、运行时后端、硬件利用率、DVFS及热变化等多因素影响，准确预测延迟是高效部署的关键挑战。

Method: 将每个推理请求表示为硬件-运行时-模型-提示四元组配置；将推理过程分离为预填充和解码两个独立阶段；设计门控预测模型，自适应融合静态描述符（模型、提示特征）与动态硬件遥测（利用率、频率、温度等）进行延迟预测。

Result: 在Pixel 8上，总延迟R²从0.953提升至0.960，解码延迟R²从0.957提升至0.973；Pixel 8 Pro预填充延迟R²从-1.383提升至0.966。跨设备迁移中，轻量校准使Pixel 8 Pro→8的总延迟R²从-0.974提升至0.940，解码延迟R²从-1.085提升至0.927。异构测试显示相同模型在不同设备上吞吐量差异显著（8.42 vs 64.38 tokens/s）。

Conclusion: 运行时感知的延迟预测结合轻量校准可显著降低性能分析开销，有效支持LLM在异构边缘平台上的延迟感知部署决策。

Abstract: Accurate latency prediction is critical for deploying large language models (LLMs) on heterogeneous edge devices, where inference latency is affected by model architecture, prompt behavior, runtime backend, hardware utilization, dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS), and thermal variation. This paper presents a runtime-aware latency prediction framework for deployment-oriented LLM selection. The framework represents each inference request as a hardware-runtime-model-prompt configuration, separates inference into prefill and decode phases, and adaptively fuses static descriptors with dynamic hardware telemetry through a gated prediction model.
  We evaluate the framework using Pixel mobile devices and validate the profiling pipeline on Jetson Nano, Orange Pi 5 Pro, and an RTX 3090-class GPU platform. On Pixel 8, the full predictor improves total-latency R-squared from 0.953 to 0.960 and decode-latency R-squared from 0.957 to 0.973 over a static-only baseline. On Pixel 8 Pro, it improves prefill-latency R-squared from -1.383 to 0.966. For cross-device transfer, calibration improves Pixel 8 Pro to Pixel 8 total-latency R-squared from -0.974 to 0.940 and decode-latency R-squared from -1.085 to 0.927. Heterogeneous profiling further shows that latency is highly device- and runtime-dependent: the same SmolLM2 model family reaches 8.42 tokens/s on Orange Pi 5 Pro but 64.38 tokens/s on an RTX 3090-class GPU. These results demonstrate that runtime-aware prediction with lightweight calibration can reduce profiling cost and support latency-aware LLM deployment across heterogeneous edge platforms.

</details>


### [60] [AgentKVShift: Efficient KV Cache Reuse for Agentic Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21604)
*Nilesh Prasad Pandey,Jason Kong,Lanxiang Hu,Quanling Zhao,Yujie Zhao,Onat Gungor,Hao Zhang,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentKVShift是一种无需训练的、基于探针引导的KV残差校正方法，通过分解记忆级偏移和token级波动，仅刷新10-30%的KV缓存即可达到接近全重计算的性能，在长时对话和智能体应用中实现2-3.5倍预填充加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无训练KV重用方法是为RAG风格原始段落设计的，在处理结构化智能体记忆时性能下降严重，无法有效应对LLM智能体记忆系统中由元数据（摘要、关键词、标签）构成的KV缓存重用问题。

Method: 提出AgentKVShift，一种探针引导的KV残差校正方法。核心发现是每个记忆单元KV重用残差可分解为共享的记忆级偏移加上小的token级波动。通过从小探针集合中估计该偏移，用单一加权校正修正每个重用token。不同于仅选择性重计算部分token而让其余缓存过时的方法，AgentKVShift同时校正未重计算的token，将刷新预算转化为整个块的可用信号。

Result: 在4个开源LLM（3B-32B参数）和两个长时记忆基准上，仅刷新10-30%缓存即可达到近全重计算性能；达到该性能所需重计算量比基线方法低5倍（基线需要45-55%刷新）；预填充加速2-3.5倍（单A100）；与KV量化正交组合，在2-bit和4-bit设置下F1超过基线方法2倍以上。

Conclusion: AgentKVShift有效解决了结构化智能体记忆中KV重用性能退化问题，通过残差校正策略在极低刷新率下实现近全重计算性能，且与量化技术兼容，为长上下文LLM智能体推理加速提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents maintain context across hundreds of interactions through agentic memory systems that actively curate retrieved content with LLM-generated metadata such as summaries, keywords, and tags. From an inference cost standpoint, every retrieval triggers a full re-encoding of these structured memory units into Key-Value (KV) states, which dominates prefill latency. Existing training-free KV reuse methods mitigate this by selectively recomputing a small fraction of tokens, but were designed for RAG-style raw passages and degrade on structured agentic memories. We present AgentKVShift, a training-free, probe-guided KV residual correction method that operates per retrieved memory unit. One of the crucial insights we demonstrate is that the per-memory KV reuse residual decomposes into a shared memory-level offset plus small token-wise fluctuations. Estimating this offset from a small probe set allows us to correct every reused token by a single weighted correction. Unlike prior reuse methods which decide which tokens to recompute and leave the rest of the cache stale, AgentKVShift also corrects the tokens it does not recompute, turning the refresh budget into useful signal across the entire chunk. Across four open-source LLMs spanning 3B to 32B parameters and two long-horizon agentic memory benchmarks (long-term dialogue and agentic applications), AgentKVShift achieves near full recompute performance while refreshing only 10-30% of the cache, outperforming baselines at the same recompute ratio. It requires up to 5x lower recompute to reach this near-full performance, which prior reuse methods only attain at 45-55% refresh. In this regime, AgentKVShift delivers prefill speedups of 2-3.5x over no-KV-reuse on a single A100. AgentKVShift orthogonally composes with KV cache quantization, retaining over 2x the F1 of prior reuse methods under aggressive 2- and 4-bit settings.

</details>


### [61] [TILT: Improving Compositional Generation in Diffusion Models with a Model-Intrinsic Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21606)
*Debottam Dutta,Jaehoon Hahm,Jianchong Chen,Romit Roy Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TILT是一种无需训练的测试时奖励对齐框架，通过定义内在奖励和KL约束目标，引导扩散采样生成更符合复杂组合提示的图像，在T2ICompBench上提升了组合对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成模型在处理复杂组合性提示时存在 faithfulness 不足的问题，需要开发测试时方法修改采样轨迹以生成更符合提示的图像。

Method: 将组合失败解释为联合分布与单概念分布之间的重叠模式，定义无需外部监督的内在奖励来偏好所有概念同时存在的样本，构建KL约束目标得到闭式倾斜目标分布和 principled guiding steps，并提出了两种引导策略及混合方法。

Result: 在T2ICompBench上的实验表明，该方法在保持图像质量的同时，相比以往基线方法提升了组合对齐性能。

Conclusion: TILT通过测试时奖励对齐有效改善了组合性文本到图像生成的对齐效果，且无需额外训练或外部奖励模型。

Abstract: Recent advances in powerful text-to-image generation models have made it increasingly important to develop test-time methods that modify the sampling trajectory to produce images more faithful to complex compositional prompts. We present TILT, a training-free framework for compositional text-to-image generation via test-time reward alignment. We interpret compositional failures as overlap modes between joint and single-concept distributions, and define a reward that favors samples where all concepts are jointly present. This reward is intrinsic to the base model and does not require any external supervision or reward models. This yields a KL-constrained objective with a closed-form tilted target distribution and principled guiding steps for diffusion sampling. The interaction of concept distributions together with the above reward naturally leads to two different guidance strategies while a hybrid approach that balances their respective benefits produces stronger performance. Experiments on prompts from T2ICompBench show that our method improves compositional alignment while preserving image quality compared to previous baselines.

</details>


### [62] [Spectral Flow Certificates for Depth-Aware Long-Range Propagation in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21607)
*Ranjan Veerabhadraswamy,Ajith Jubilson Emerson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出频谱流证书（SFCs），通过在图归一化拉普拉斯矩阵上计算单个标量，无需模型训练或标签数据，即可预测图神经网络在长程任务上的训练精度。SFC融合了图的代数连通性和消息传递深度，比传统结构统计量（如平均有效电阻、图直径）解释两倍以上的精度方差，在25种合成图和150种真实分子图上解释力超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GNN部署在新图上时，无法在训练前低成本判断图结构是否允许信息在远距离节点间有效传播，即缺乏一个无需训练即可诊断拓扑瓶颈的预筛选工具。

Method: 提出频谱流证书（SFC），从图的归一化拉普拉斯矩阵计算单个标量，融合代数连通性与选择的消自传递深度，衡量在给定深度预算内能跨越临界频谱瓶颈的程度。

Result: 在25种合成图族和150个真实分子图拓扑上，SFC在训练前预测GNN长程精度，解释力超过90%；相比平均有效电阻和图直径等经典结构统计量，解释两倍以上的精度方差。

Conclusion: 仅需一次特征值计算即可在昂贵训练流程之前标记拓扑受限的图，为GNN部署提供原则性的第一道筛选。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks propagate information through local message passing, but the graph topologies themselves can silently prevent any amount of training from solving long-range tasks. When we deploy GNNs on new graphs, there is currently no inexpensive way to know, before training begins, whether the graphs' structures will allow information to travel far enough between distant nodes. We address this gap by proposing Spectral Flow Certificates (SFCs), single scalars computed from the graphs' normalised Laplacians in seconds, requiring no model training and no labelled data. An SFC fuses a graph's algebraic connectivity with the chosen message-passing depth into one number that measures how much of the critical spectral bottleneck can be traversed within the available depth budget. Unlike raw spectral gaps, which are static and depth-agnostic, SFCs adapt as the number of layers increases and therefore carry strictly more diagnostic information when depths vary. Compared with classical structural statistics such as average effective resistance and graph diameter, SFCs explain more than twice as much variance in trained GNN long-range accuracy. Across twenty-five synthetic graph families spanning paths, cycles, grids, regular graphs, and random graphs, SFCs predict trained accuracy before any gradients are computed, achieving explanatory power above ninety percent at all tested depths. The same predictive relationships hold on one hundred fifty real molecular graph topologies drawn from three independent benchmark datasets, confirming that the findings are not artefacts of their synthetic construction. Taken together, these results show that a single eigenvalue computation is sufficient to flag topology-limited graphs before committing to expensive training pipelines, providing a principled first filter for GNN deployments.

</details>


### [63] [Coupled Hierarchical Search over Topology and Execution for Agentic Workflow Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21609)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HierFlow是一种无需训练、测试时层次化搜索架构，通过融合反馈引导的拓扑调整与MCTS启发的树搜索，自动设计智能体工作流，在问答、数学推理和代码生成任务中实现了高质量与计算效率的最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化工作流虽能增强LLM处理复杂问题的能力，但其自动创建受限于巨大的组合搜索空间，现有方法依赖离线训练，导致灵活性差且资源消耗大。

Method: 将工作流生成建模为拓扑与执行交织的搜索范式，提出HierFlow：无训练、测试时层次化搜索架构。采用反馈引导的拓扑调整和MCTS启发的子工作流树搜索，并通过智能门控模块按需触发执行级搜索，同时分析了跨任务耦合程度对层次化拆分效果的影响。

Result: 在问答、数学推理和代码生成基准测试中，HierFlow持续优于强基线方法，在高质量结果与计算效率之间实现了最优平衡，且无需额外训练开销。

Conclusion: HierFlow通过层次化拓扑与执行联合搜索，有效解决了工作流自动化中的组合爆炸问题，为测试时自动化工作流设计提供了一种高效、灵活的无需训练方案。

Abstract: Although structured workflows empower Large Language Models (LLMs) to tackle complex problems, automating their creation is severely hindered by a vast combinatorial search space, frequently resulting in inflexible and resource-heavy offline training dependencies. To address this, we conceptualize workflow generation as an intertwined topology-and-execution search paradigm, where the broader topological layer dictates subtask boundaries and lower-level execution outcomes actively reshape the topology itself. Building on this foundation, we introduce HierFlow, a training-free, test-time hierarchical search architecture that automates agentic workflow design by merging feedback-guided topology adjustments with a fast, MCTS-inspired tree search for sub-workflow optimization. HierFlow maximizes efficiency through an intelligent gating module that selectively triggers execution-level searches based on contextual necessity, a mechanism we further support with an in-depth analysis detailing how varying degrees of cross-task coupling impact the effectiveness of hierarchical splitting. Comprehensive testing across question answering, mathematical reasoning, and code generation benchmarks confirms that HierFlow consistently outperforms strong baselines, delivering an optimal balance of high-quality results and computational efficiency without any additional training overhead.

</details>


### [64] [SCOPE and SCION: A Benchmark and an Auditable Reference Pipeline for Schema Induction and Fusion from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21610)
*Miaobo Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Shuhao Hu,Bokun Wang,Rui Chen,Xin Wang,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE基准（24个信息抽取源的模式图构建评测）和SCION流水线（基于证据的可审计模式构建方法），实验表明SCION-lite在多项指标上超越现有基线，SCION-RL减少了对闭源大模型的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息抽取和知识图谱构建系统大多假设模式图（schema graph）已预先存在，但模式图的实际构建本身是上游瓶颈。缺乏从原始文本到模式图的标准化评测基准和可复现的构建方法。

Method: 构建SCOPE基准：从15个关系抽取和9个事件抽取公开源归一化为24组黄金模式图；提出SCION流水线：从训练文本构建候选空间，在严格JSON契约下进行命名、合并、过滤、验证和保守融合，所有操作均链接到证据。

Result: 在SCOPE核心套件上，SCION-lite在字面、模糊、连续和图四种模式图指标上均取得最高F1；SCION-RL（紧凑开源模型）降低了对闭源大模型的依赖。

Conclusion: SCION方法在模式构建任务上表现优于现有基线，但作者强调结果是面向标准化目标而非声称自动构建超越人工本体设计；项目已开源全部代码、日志和评测包。

Abstract: Schema graphs are an upstream bottleneck of schema-grounded information extraction and knowledge graph construction, yet most extraction systems assume the schema is already available. We introduce SCOPE (Schema Construction and Ontology-induction Pipeline Evaluation), a train-text-only benchmark for corpus-to-schema induction and optional schema fusion from raw text, built from 24 public information extraction sources (15 RE and 9 EE) normalized into evaluation-only gold schema graphs; its core event-extraction target covers event types and within-event argument roles, with inter-event links reported separately. We present SCION (Schema Construction and Induction with Ontology Normalization), an auditable reference pipeline rather than a new extraction architecture; it constructs candidate spaces from train text and restricts naming, merging, filtering, validation, and conservative fusion to candidate-linked evidence under strict JSON contracts. On the SCOPE core suite, SCION-lite attains the highest F1 among released source-schema references, Text2Onto-style, LLM-only, and matched extract-then-aggregate baselines under Literal, Fuzzy, Continuous, and Graph schema-graph metrics, while the compact open-model SCION-RL variant reduces reliance on proprietary LLM schema engineers. These results are reported against normalized typed-edge targets rather than as claims that induced schemas surpass human ontology design; the release includes evidence-linked outputs, parse/fallback logs, candidate retention/merging logs, run manifests, code, and benchmark packages at https://github.com/wandugu/paper_scion.

</details>


### [65] [Procedural Knowledge Is Not Low-Rank: Why LoRA Fails to Internalize Multi-Step Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21612)
*Simon Dennis,Kevin Shabahang,Hao Guo,Rivaan Patil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LoRA在程序性知识任务（如多步骤流程跟踪）上无法匹敌全量微调，即使提高秩也效果有限，因为全量微调的权重更新有效秩远高于LoRA的预设范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究参数高效微调方法LoRA在程序性知识任务上的表现是否与全量微调相当，验证其能否适用于需要多步骤推理和条件分支的智能体应用场景。

Method: 在旅行预订（14节点）、Zoom支持（14节点）和保险理赔（55节点）三个领域，对3B和8B模型进行系统消融实验（r=16-128），对比LoRA与全量微调的任务成功率，并进行SVD分析权重更新的有效秩。

Result: 所有LoRA配置在程序性任务上均显著低于全量微调（任务成功率≤2.54 vs 4.11，p<0.001），即使提高秩到128也无法弥合差距；SVD分析显示全量微调权重更新的有效秩在761-1026之间，秩128仅能捕获43-51%的Frobenius范数。

Conclusion: 对于程序性知识任务，LoRA存在根本性局限，无法替代全量微调，这对智能体应用构成重要限制。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like LoRA have become the default for adapting large language models, succeeding across instruction following, style transfer, and factual adaptation. We show that for procedural knowledge--the ability to follow multi-step procedures with conditional branching through to terminal states--LoRA fails to match full fine-tuning at the ranks where it retains its efficiency advantage. In a systematic ablation (r = 16--128) on a procedural travel booking task (14 nodes), all LoRA configurations fail uniformly (task success <= 2.54 vs. 4.11 for full fine-tuning, all p < 0.001), with scores decreasing at higher ranks--despite maintaining 95--99% conversation completion rates. Cross-domain replication on Zoom support (14 nodes) and insurance claims (55 nodes) at 8B confirms the failure generalizes: LoRA underperforms full fine-tuning by 0.8--2.2 points on average at both r = 32 and r = 128, with the largest gap on the most complex procedure. Quadrupling rank from 32 to 128 provides marginal improvement but does not close the gap. SVD analysis of the weight changes produced by full fine-tuning explains why: across three domains at both 3B and 8B, the mean effective rank of the update ranges from 761 to 1,026, and rank 128 captures only 43--51% of the squared Frobenius norm. Together, these findings establish that for procedural tasks LoRA falls well short of full fine-tuning--a fundamental limitation for agentic applications.

</details>


### [66] [The Hard Decision Layer: Evidence for Committed Inference in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21613)
*Ashwath Vaithinathan Aravindan,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer-based语言模型在多选题问答中存在一个"硬决策层"(HDL)，即答案选项排名在该层突然稳定下来的自然架构属性。该现象在四种模型(Qwen、Llama、Granite、Mistral)和四个基准数据集上一致出现，无需学习路由策略，且对微调具有不变性。HDL处准确率提升显著(最高+0.61)，之后趋于稳定。消融实验证实该现象源于模型架构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer语言模型在多选题问答任务中何时及如何对预测做出决策，揭示模型内部推理过程的机制性理解。

Method: 识别"硬决策层"(HDL)作为模型架构属性，通过分析各层中选项排名的稳定性变化来确定HDL位置；在四种模型(Qwen、Llama、Granite、Mistral)和四个基准数据集上进行实证验证；通过微调不变性实验和消融实验(标签格式、问题复杂度)进行系统性分析。

Result: HDL在所有测试模型和数据集中一致出现，无需学习路由策略；HDL对微调具有不变性；HDL处准确率提升显著，最高达+0.61(Qwen在CommonsenseQA上)，之后性能趋于稳定。

Conclusion: HDL是Transformer架构固有的属性，为理解Transformer推理机制提供了新的视角，并可能用于高效推理和模型引导。

Abstract: We investigate where and how transformer-based language models commit to predictions in multiple-choice question answering. We identify the _Hard Decision Layer_ (HDL), a natural architectural property where answer option rankings stabilize abruptly during inference. Empirical validation across four language models (Qwen, Llama, Granite, Mistral) and four benchmark datasets demonstrates consistent HDL emergence without learned routing policies. We also show that the HDL is invariant to fine-tuning. Our results reveal striking accuracy improvements at the HDL: up to +0.61 (Qwen on CommonsenseQA), after which performance stabilizes. Systematic ablations on label formats and problem complexity confirm the phenomenon is fundamental to model architecture. These findings offer mechanistic insights into transformer inference and suggest opportunities for efficient reasoning and model steering. All code and results required to reproduce this work are available in https://github.com/Mystic-Slice/hard-decision-layer

</details>


### [67] [Household Movement Detection in Mixed-Format Occupancy Data Using LLM-Based Entity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21614)
*Sasirekha Oguri,John R. Talburt,Mert Can Cakmak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI增强框架，通过结合LLM命名实体识别、语义嵌入和基于图的推理，从未标准化的姓名-地址数据中检测与家庭迁移相关的间接实体链接，在SPX基准数据集上相比强基线方法F1分数提升6-8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的实体解析方法依赖记录间的成对相似度比较，无法捕捉人口居住数据中家庭迁移产生的间接关系。由于数据格式混杂、噪声、重复及缺少稳定标识符，检测这类模式十分困难。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于提示的大语言模型命名实体识别，无需大量预处理即可提取个人姓名和地址；(2) 语义文本嵌入用于稳健的相似度计算；(3) 基于图的推理推断群体级别的迁移模式。

Result: 在SPX基准数据集（S8-S12）上的实验表明，引入间接家庭迁移证据使召回率提升8-15%，同时保持高精度，F1分数相比强成对基线方法提高6-8%。

Conclusion: 该框架有效利用了家庭迁移产生的间接关系来改进实体解析，在非标准化数据上取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Entity resolution (ER) typically relies on pairwise similarity comparisons between records, which limits its ability to capture indirect relationships present in demographic occupancy data. An important indirect pattern arises from household movement, where multiple individuals relocate together across addresses, but detecting such patterns is difficult due to mixed-format records, noise, duplication, and the absence of stable identifiers. This paper proposes an AI-enhanced framework for detecting indirect entity links associated with household movement in unstandardized name-address data. The approach integrates prompt-based large language model (LLM) named entity recognition for extracting personal names and addresses without extensive preprocessing, semantic text embeddings for robust similarity computation, and graph-based reasoning to infer group-level movement patterns. Experimental evaluation on SPX benchmark datasets (S8-S12) generated using the Synthetic Occupancy Generator demonstrates that incorporating indirect household movement evidence improves recall by 8-15% while maintaining high precision, yielding F1-score gains of 6-8% over a strong pairwise baseline.

</details>


### [68] [FrED: External Data Influence Estimation via Domain Knowledge Graph Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21615)
*Theodoros Aivalis,Iraklis A. Klampanos,Antonis Troumpoukis,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全在黑盒环境下运行的训练数据归因概率框架，通过融合连续特征相似性与离散领域知识图谱，在无需模型内部参数的情况下实现高效、可解释的数据影响分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速部署使得训练数据归因对确保透明度和问责制至关重要，但现有参数化方法需要计算代价高昂的模型权重访问，而基于相似性的方法忽略了深层结构上下文。

Method: 提出一个概率框架，融合连续特征相似性与离散的领域特定知识图谱(KGs)，在完全黑盒设置下运行，显式奖励高特异性历史样本并抑制通用背景数据。

Result: 在抽象艺术图像合成领域，线性数据建模分数超过标准黑盒相似性基线，大幅缩小了与梯度估计器的差距；在环境预测领域，利用领域知识图谱检索物理一致的历史类比，改善了区域洪水预测的地理定位。

Conclusion: 该框架无需内部模型访问，为事后影响分析和领域导向检索提供了高效、可解释的机制。

Abstract: The rapid deployment of generative AI has amplified the critical need for Training Data Attribution to ensure transparency and accountability. However, current parametric approaches require computationally prohibitive access to model weights, while similarity-based methods ignore deep structural context. We propose a novel probabilistic framework that operates entirely in a black-box setting. Our method fuses continuous feature similarities with discrete, domain-specific Knowledge Graphs (KGs). This approach ensures the attribution is grounded in structural reality, explicitly rewarding highly specific historical samples while preventing generic background data from dominating the results. We evaluate our framework across two distinct domains where linking outputs to data and domain context is inherently complex: abstract artistic image synthesis and high-dimensional physical weather forecasting. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates the robust efficacy of our approach. In the artistic domain, it achieves a strong Linear Datamodeling Score that exceeds standard black-box similarity baselines, while closing much of the gap to gradient-based estimators. We additionally present a cross-domain feasibility case study in environmental forecasting, where we use domain KGs to retrieve physically consistent historical analogs for regional flood forecasts, improving geographic localisation over a latent-only baseline. Operating entirely without internal model access, our approach provides an efficient, interpretable mechanism for post-hoc influence analysis and domain-grounded retrieval.

</details>


### [69] [Lost in Context: Addressing Context Anxiety in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21616)
*Ifueko Igbinedion,Jillian Ross,Etienne Ricardez,Sertac Karaman,Eric So*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 前沿推理模型在解决超出其能力范围的问题时，会因“上下文焦虑”（过早自我怀疑）导致失败，这源于模型无法准确估算完成任务所需的token数量，并造成效率损失。研究表明，模型可以学习不表现上下文焦虑的替代策略，提示性能提升可通过改进模型对自身局限性的评估与适应实现，而非仅依赖能力扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示并系统研究推理模型在具备解决问题能力时，因过早自我怀疑（上下文焦虑）而失败的现象，探索其成因及影响。

Method: 首次对上下文焦虑进行系统性研究，通过分析模型估算所需token能力的不准确性，证明该现象部分源于此，并对比模型在有无感知约束下的效率差异。

Result: 上下文焦虑确实导致模型在具备能力时失败，且与token估算不准确相关；该现象在感知约束下造成显著的效率损失。

Conclusion: 模型可以通过学习替代策略避免上下文焦虑，性能提升不必然依赖能力扩展，而可通过改进模型对自身局限性的评估与适应来实现。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom suggests that reasoning models fail when problems exceed their capabilities. However, we find that frontier reasoning models sometimes possess the necessary capabilities to solve problems but fail due to premature self-doubt -- a phenomenon informally known as context anxiety. We provide the first systematic study of context anxiety, demonstrating that it arises, in part, from a model's inability to accurately estimate the tokens required to complete a task. We also show that context anxiety leads to material efficiency losses when models operate under perceived constraints. Building on this analysis, we further show that models can learn alternative strategies for solving long-horizon problems without exhibiting context anxiety, suggesting that performance improvements may be achievable not through scaling model capabilities, but by improving models' ability to accurately assess and adapt to their own limitations.

</details>


### [70] [Do VLMs Read or Rewrite? On Transcription Faithfulness in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21617)
*Gwang Gook Lee,Kenan Emir Ak,Jay Mohta,Yan Xu,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FaithC4多语言扰动基准（英/中/韩共1,455页），系统评估15个系统在文本不完美时的转录忠实性。发现通用VLM在扰动下WER最多增加4.5点，而传统OCR仅增加0.6点。通过逐层探针揭示重写机制：仅当扰动词的最终层FFN表示接近原始编码时才会发生重写；短词（4-6字符）重写率高达10%，8字符以上骤降为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR基准基于清洁文本，无法检测VLM在文本不完美时倾向于将其"修正"为更合理形式的非忠实转录行为。作者旨在量化并解释这一现象。

Method: 构建FaithC4基准（含三种扰动：乱序、随机替换、视觉相似替换），评估三类系统（通用VLM、OCR专用VLM、传统OCR管线）；对Qwen3-VL-4B进行逐层探针分析FFN表示。

Result: 通用VLM在英语扰动下WER退化最多4.5点，OCR专用VLM退化0.2-2点，传统OCR退化<0.6点；重写仅当扰动词末层FFN表示接近原始编码时发生；短词（4-6字符）重写率高达10%，8字符以上为0%。

Conclusion: VLM并非忠实的转录器，在文本不完美时存在重写偏差；该行为与内部表示的距离和词长高度相关；传统OCR在扰动下更为稳健。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used in place of traditional OCR pipelines for document understanding. In this paper, we show they do not always act as faithful transcribers: when text is imperfect, they often tend to rewrite it into a more plausible form - a behavior that clean-text OCR benchmarks cannot detect. We introduce FaithC4, a multilingual perturbation benchmark of 1,455 single-page documents (English, Chinese, Korean) with three perturbation families: scramble, random substitution, and visually similar substitution. We use the benchmark to evaluate 15 systems spanning general-purpose VLMs, OCR-specialized VLMs, and traditional OCR pipelines. These three categories differ in WER degradation under perturbation: general-purpose VLMs degrade by up to 4.5 points, OCR-specialized VLMs by 0.2-2 points, and traditional OCR by less than 0.6 points on English. Probing Qwen3-VL-4B layer-by-layer, we identify a consistent pattern: rewriting fires only when a perturbed word's final layer FFN representation stays close to the original encoding; when the representation diverges sufficiently, the model transcribes faithfully. Word length affects rewriting rate: short words (4-6 characters) are rewritten up to 10% of the time, with a sharp cutoff at 8 characters above which rewriting drops to 0%.

</details>


### [71] [LeafData: An Agentic System for Data Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21618)
*Sadanand Katukuri,Rajasekhar Bada,Navya Induri,Rohit Gandham,Lynette Pinto,Joses Selvan,Abishek Krishnamoorthy,Joseph Rozario,Pu Tian,Pavan Poudel,Yalong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeafData是一个智能代理系统，通过前端聊天机器人逐步收集用户意图并执行模式驱动验证，后端服务生成可直接用于编排平台的可执行JSON配置，实现跨异构数据源（关系型数据库、文件系统、文档数据库、REST API）的无代码数据迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据迁移依赖用户手动编写JSON配置来定义数据连接、管道逻辑和编排行为，这需要用户具备领域知识，既耗时又容易出错。

Method: LeafData采用前后端分离的智能代理架构：前端聊天机器人逐步收集用户需求并进行模式驱动的输入验证；后端服务将验证后的输入处理生成JSON配置工件，这些工件可直接被编排平台消费，实现端到端的管道生成与执行。

Result: 系统能够将用户意图转化为经过验证且可执行的JSON配置，支持跨关系型数据库、文件系统、文档数据库和REST API等多种异构数据源与连接器的数据迁移，无需手动编码即可完成全流程。

Conclusion: LeafData通过对话式交互和自动化配置生成，显著降低了数据迁移的领域知识门槛，减少了手动配置的错误率和时间成本。

Abstract: Modern data migration relies on JSON configuration to define data connection, pipeline logic, and orchestration behavior. This requires domain knowledge from users and is time-consuming and error-prone. In this paper, we present LeafData, an agentic system that converts user intent into validated and executable JSON configuration for data migration. Specifically, LeafData comprises a frontend chatbot and the backend service. The chatbot incrementally collects required information from users and performs schema-driven validation, while the backend service processes validated inputs and generates JSON configuration artifacts.
